*Hong-Fei Li,Xi-Lin Gao,Yi-Juan Xiang,Shu-Song Huang,Yi-lin Wang,Chun-Dong Xue,Zhuo Yang,Yong-Jiang Li,Xu-Qu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稳定性引导的分析框架，用于解耦微流体连续拉伸流中的流体动力学伪影与真实力学表型特征。通过构建93维特征空间并采用跨流动筛选策略，将流动相关方差从69.9%降至9.3%，在保持诊断保真度的同时实现了流无关的连续动态表型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微流体装置中的复杂微通道（如双曲几何结构）虽能揭示细胞在连续拉伸应力下的瞬态变形动力学，但产生的高维特征空间极易受流体动力学伪影影响——流速变化会扭曲判别边界，使特征分布反映的是流体条件而非内在生物学特性。现有方法缺乏有效手段将流动诱导噪声与真实力学表型信号解耦。

Method: 构建93维特征空间（涵盖形态动力学、运动学和细胞内光学密度轨迹）；提出跨流动筛选策略（基于结构一致性和统计持久性）提取稳健描述符；分别生成二分类（20特征）和癌症亚型分类（25特征）的自适应特征子集；通过方差归因分析验证流动条件伪影的中和效果。

Result: 流动相关方差在主成分中从69.9%降至9.3%（亚型分类任务）；宏观二元区分依赖整体运动学转变，而克隆亚型区分需要局部细胞内光学异质性；优化特征子集在多种机器学习架构和受限采样条件下保持诊断保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了稳健的、流无关的连续动态表型分析基础，有效消除了流体动力学伪影对力学表型特征的干扰，为高通量单细胞力学表型分析提供了可靠的方法学支撑。

Abstract: Label-free, image-based cellular mechanophenotyping in microfluidic devices provides a high-throughput method for single-cell profiling. However, while complex microchannels (e.g., hyperbolic geometries) reveal transient deformation dynamics under continuous extensional stress, the resulting high-dimensional feature spaces are highly susceptible to hydrodynamic artifacts. Flow rate variations often distort discriminative boundaries, linking feature distributions to fluid conditions rather than intrinsic biology. To overcome this, we introduce a stability-guided analytical framework that decouples flow-induced noise from authentic mechanobiological signatures. We tracked the morphodynamic, kinematic, and intracellular optical-density trajectories of healthy and malignant ovarian cells to build a 93-dimensional feature space. Using a cross-flow screening strategy based on structural consistency and statistical persistence, we isolated robust descriptors, creating task-adapted subsets (20 features for binary classification; 25 for cancer subtyping). Variance-attribution analysis confirmed the neutralization of flow-conditioned artifacts; notably, flow-associated variance in the primary principal component fell from 69.9% to 9.3% in the subtyping task. We also found that macroscopic binary discrimination depends on bulk kinematic transitions, while clonal subtyping requires localized intracellular optical heterogeneity. These optimized subsets maintained diagnostic fidelity across multiple machine learning architectures and restricted sampling conditions. This framework establishes a robust, flow-independent foundation for continuous dynamic phenotyping.

</details>


### [18] [Arbor: Explicit Geometric Conditioning for Controllable 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23514)
*Jan-Niklas Dihlmann,Andreas Engelhardt,Simon Donne,Hendrik P. A. Lensch,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Arbor 是一种可训练的附加模块，通过约束网格（包括 hull、avoidance、touch 三种区域）作为原生 3D 控制接口，在冻结的潜在扩散模型中实现文本条件下的可控 3D 生成，无需专用损失函数即可提升约束服从性，同时保持生成质量和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本/图像条件 3D 生成模型难以直接控制物体应占据或避让的空间区域（如椅子需适配坐姿包络、道具需留出运动间隙），而文本提示和图像视图无法有效传达这类空间意图，亟需一种显式的 3D 控制接口。

Method: 将约束网格（hull/avoidance/touch 区域）转换为 tokens，作为原生 3D 控制信号注入冻结的潜在去噪器中；设计路由机制（routed attachment），使每个潜在区域仅接收与其空间位置相关的约束部分，从而保持几何信号的局部性。

Result: 在自动和艺术家策划的约束控制基准上评估 hull、avoidance、touch 三类约束，并与用户偏好研究进行对比。结果表明，即使没有专用的服从性损失函数，Arbor 也能显著提升约束服从性，同时保持生成质量和多样性。

Conclusion: Arbor 证明了将约束网格作为原生 3D 控制接口的有效性，为文本条件 3D 生成提供了精确的空间控制能力，弥补了现有方法在空间意图传达上的不足。

Abstract: Text and image conditioned 3D models now generate convincing assets, but they still offer little direct control over the space an object should occupy or avoid. In authoring, this spatial intent is often known before generation starts. A chair should fit a seating envelope, a prop should leave clearance for motion, or a part should expose a contact surface. Prompts and image views are poor carriers for such constraints, requiring the need for an explicit control interface.
  We present Arbor, a trainable attachment for text conditioned latent 3D generation. Arbor introduces constraint meshes as a native 3D control interface. The interface uses hull regions where geometry should exist, avoidance regions that should remain empty, and touch regions the object should contact. Unlike completion or whole object scaffold control, these meshes are not target evidence. They are local typed requirements and can include regions where no surface should appear. Arbor keeps this signal as geometry by converting constraint meshes into tokens and learning a routed attachment inside a frozen denoiser. Each latent region can therefore receive the part of the constraint that matters for its spatial location.
  We evaluate Arbor on automatic and artist curated control benchmarks with hull, avoidance, and touch constraints, and compare the metric trends to a user preference study. Even without dedicated compliance losses, Arbor improves constraint obedience while preserving object quality and variation under fixed constraints.

</details>


### [19] [MotionPyramid: Hierarchical Motion Representation and Residual Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20705)
*Gao Zhu,Zaishuo Xia,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionPyramid提出了一种层次化运动表征，能从运动数据中学习从低级动作命令到高级运动程序的多级结构，并作为可复用的动作接口供下游强化学习策略使用，实现了类似感知层次的组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究运动控制中是否存在类似于感知领域从局部边缘到整体对象的表征层次结构，即从低级即时动作命令到高级长时程行为（如接触、步态、平衡恢复、抓取等）的层次化组织方式。

Method: 从运动跟踪教师模型出发，训练递归的潜在解码器堆栈：低级潜在变量解码为即时全身动作命令，高级潜在变量通过低级层展开为时间上扩展的运动程序。预训练后冻结层次结构，下游强化学习策略将其作为不同控制分辨率的动作接口。另提出Residual Interfaces，允许策略通过冻结层次结构同时维护粗粒度、段级和帧级的残差命令。

Result: 粗粒度接口通过将探索约束在结构化段内来改善早期学习和运动规律性；细粒度接口保留反馈控制和最终任务精度。表征探测显示层次结构支持遍历、插值、转换和定性组合。Residual Interfaces使粗粒度运动程序与细粒度残差修正得以在单一控制器中共存。

Conclusion: 运动可以像感知一样组织成可复用的多级表征，在提供结构化抽象的同时不牺牲可控性。

Abstract: We ask whether the representational hierarchy seen in perception, from local primitives such as edges to higher level structures such as parts and objects, can be established for motion. In humanoid control, low level actions specify immediate motor commands, while meaningful behavior is organized over longer temporal scales, including contacts, gait fragments, balance recovery, reaching, and whole body skills. We introduce MotionPyramid, a hierarchical action representation that learns such structure from motion data. Starting from a motion tracking teacher, it trains a recursive stack of latent decoders: low level latents decode to immediate full body motor commands, while higher level latents unfold through lower levels into temporally extended motion programs. After pretraining, the hierarchy is frozen and reused by downstream reinforcement learning policies as a family of action interfaces at different control resolutions. Experiments show the learned levels form a motion hierarchy: coarser interfaces improve early learning and motion regularity by constraining exploration to structured segments, while finer interfaces preserve feedback control and final task precision. Representation probes show the hierarchy supports traversal, interpolation, transition, and qualitative composition, exposing editable control handles across temporal scales. Finally, we introduce Residual Interfaces, letting a downstream policy maintain coarse, segment level, and frame level residual commands through the frozen hierarchy. Analogous to residual or skip connections in deep networks, this allows coarse motion programs and fine residual corrections to coexist within one controller. MotionPyramid shows that motion, like perception, can be organized into a reusable multi level representation, providing structured abstraction without sacrificing controllability.

</details>


### [20] [Semantic Browsing: Controllable Diversity for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23679)
*Sara Dorfman,Maya Vishnevsky,Omer Dahary,Or Patashnik,Daniel Cohen-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控多样性方法，通过在文本层面而非像素层面引入结构化变化，实现语义浏览——用户可以系统性地遍历有意义的、可解释的语义轴，从而在生成图像中获得多样且可导航的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在视觉保真度和提示遵循方面表现优异，但生成样本趋于单一视觉解释，缺乏多样性。现有多样性方法产生的输出由偶然变化驱动，而非有意义的设计选择，因此需要一种在生成样本上施加结构约束的多样性方法。

Method: 利用现代文本到图像模型在详细描述上训练的特性，将语义决策与像素生成解耦，直接在文本层面引入多样性。通过丰富的文本表示，让视觉语言模型(VLM)在完整场景上下文中操作，并采用代理工作流显式地施加与原始提示相适应的结构化变化。

Result: 该方法能够生成多样且可导航的设计空间，其中每个变化都对应一个具体的、用户可理解的语义决策。

Conclusion: 通过将多样性控制从像素层面转移到文本层面，并借助VLM和代理工作流，可以实现在生成图像中进行有结构的语义浏览，为用户提供富有创造性的探索体验。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image models excel in visual fidelity and prompt adherence. However, this strict adherence comes at the cost of diversity: generated samples tend to collapse into a single visual interpretation. Existing methods to improve diversity produce outputs driven by incidental variations rather than meaningful design choices. This motivates a new variant of the diversity task where structure is enforced on the generated samples. We introduce a method for controlled diversity that enables Semantic Browsing, where users can navigate structured image galleries and experience creative exploration through a systematic traversal of meaningful, interpretable axes of variation. Achieving this level of semantic control requires a deep understanding of the scene. We exploit the fact that recent text-to-image models are trained on elaborated captions, effectively decoupling semantic decision-making from pixel generation. This enables a paradigm shift: instead of relying on stochastic variation within the text-to-image model, we induce diversity directly at the text level. By leveraging rich textual representations, we allow a Vision Language Model (VLM) to operate on the full scene context. To overcome the generic outputs typical of standard VLMs, we employ an agentic workflow that explicitly enforces structured variation attuned to the original prompt. We demonstrate that our method produces diverse and navigable design spaces where every variation corresponds to a specific, user-understandable semantic decision.

</details>


### [21] [GEOPHYS: The Geometry of Physical Plausibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20707)
*Christian Internò,Alexander Pondaven,Habon Issa,Fabio Pizzati,Francesco Pinto,Markus Olhofer,Ivan Laptev,Philip Torr,Eero P. Simoncelli,Barbara Hammer,David Klindt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GEOPHYS方法，利用冻结图像编码器每帧嵌入的五种几何属性来检测视频中的物理不合理性，无需外部多模态大模型或训练修改，在物理违反检测和视频生成验证上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能毫秒级识别物理不合理事件，但现有机器学习方法要么依赖外部多模态大模型作为评判器，要么需要对训练流程进行专门修改，速度慢且成本高。本文旨在探索是否可以从预训练图像编码器的时空嵌入中直接提取物理合理性信号。

Method: 提出GEOPHYS框架，从冻结图像编码器的每帧嵌入中提取五种几何属性（如轨迹连续性、空间一致性等），将这些属性聚合作为物理合理性的指示器。无需微调编码器或额外训练，仅利用预训练模型固有的几何特征。

Result: 在LikePhys上达98.3%，IntPhys2上达93.3%的物理违反检测准确率，而V-JEPA 2、GPT-4o、Gemini及12种视频扩散模型接近随机水平。作为视频生成的最佳N验证器，将MAGI-1 24B在PhysicsIQ上的准确率从50.01%提升至64.50%，同时相比V-JEPA 2世界模型验证器降低1.5倍时间和4.65倍内存。

Conclusion: 物理合理性信号已隐含在预训练图像编码器的时间嵌入的几何属性中，无需显式物理建模或大规模多模态模型即可高效检测视频物理违反，并在视频生成质量验证中展现出实用价值。

Abstract: While humans can identify physically implausible events within milliseconds, machine learning approaches addressing the same problem are extremely slow and expensive. They either rely on external multimodal-LLM judges or require ad-hoc modifications to the training procedure. In this work, we argue that indicators of physical plausibility are implicitly captured by five geometric properties of the per-frame embeddings produced by frozen image encoders. In aggregate, we call them GEOPHYS. First, we show that these signals correlate with human EEG responses to two forms of object-permanence violations. Second, GEOPHYS robustly discriminates physically implausible videos from realistic ones, achieving state-of-the-art physics-violation detection: 98.3% on LikePhys and 93.3% on IntPhys2, whereas V-JEPA 2, GPT-4o, Gemini, and twelve modern video diffusion models perform near chance. Third, used as a best-of-N verifier for physical alignment during video generation, GEOPHYS lifts MAGI-1 24B from 50.01% to 64.50% on PhysicsIQ at 1.5x lower wall-clock and 4.65x lower memory than the V-JEPA 2 world-model verifier. Ultimately, GEOPHYS demonstrates that physical plausibility in videos can be assessed by leveraging the emergent geometric properties of temporal features extracted from image encoders.

</details>


### [22] [TeleStyle V2: Beyond Content-Preserving Style Transfer with Self-Distillation and Distribution-Matching-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20709)
*Shiwen Zhang,Yifan Xu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeleStyle V2，通过自蒸馏数据合成策略和分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）技术，实现了支持多种内容-风格参考形式（写实/艺术化）的风格迁移，并解决了内容一致性退化问题，性能与商业模型gemini-3-pro-image-preview相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeleStyle V1仅支持照片级内容参考与艺术风格参考，无法处理艺术化内容参考与写实风格参考的常见场景；同时存在内容/风格参考顺序混淆问题以及内容一致性退化问题。

Method: 1) 提出自蒸馏数据合成策略（Self-Distillation data synthesis），从TeleStyle V1中构建三元组训练数据；2) 引入分布匹配蒸馏（Distribution Matching Distillation, DMD）保持基础模型的文本引导图像编辑能力，修复SFT导致的内容一致性退化；3) 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B作为VLM编码器，并引入prompt enhancer解决参考顺序混淆问题。

Result: TeleStyle V2支持四种内容-风格形式（RnR、RnS、SnR、SnS），在内容保留风格迁移任务上性能与Qwen-Image-Edit-2509-DMD相当，且与商业模型gemini-3-pro-image-preview达到可比的风格迁移效果。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏数据合成策略有效扩展了风格迁移的适用范围，DMD技术成功解决了内容一致性退化，TeleStyle V2在通用图像编辑能力上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Given a content reference and a style reference, content-preserving style transfer requires the model to generate stylized outputs with content and style consistency. We introduced TeleStyle V1 to tackle this problem. However, TeleStyle V1 is trained with photorealistic content reference and artistic style reference, which makes it incapable to cope with artistic content reference and realistic style reference in most cases. In this paper, we designed a Self-Distillation data synthesis strategy to construct such triplets from TeleStyle V1. Trained with such self-distilled triplets, our TeleStyle V2 supports Content-Style references in the forms of Realistic-and-Realistic (RnR), Realistic-and-Stylized (RnS), Stylized-and-Realistic (SnR), Stylized-and-Stylized (SnS). In addition, we found Distribution Matching Distillation could preserve the general text-guided image editing capability of the foundation model and fix the content consistency degradation caused by SFT process. Through quantitative evaluations, our TeleStyleV2-QIE-2509-DMD performs at least on par with Qwen-Image-Edit-2509-DMD, demonstrating strong general image editing skills beyond content-preserving style transfer. We observed the content/style reference order confusion problem in TeleStyle V1 and further introduced prompt enhancer to solve it. TeleStyle V2 uses Qwen-Image-Edit's VLM encoder, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, to generate content prompt and style prompt for free. TeleStyle V2 could achieve comparable style transfer performance with state-of-the-art commercial model, gemini-3-pro-image-preview.

</details>


### [23] [Video2Code: Generating Interactive Webpages from UI Videos via Action-Aware Revisit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20711)
*Mingde Xu,Zhen Yang,Yan Wang,Yu Wang,Xijun Liu,Zijun Dou,Wenyi Hong,Xiaotao Gu,Bin Xu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video2Code方法，通过动作感知的视频理解，在粗粒度分析后对关键区域进行高时间分辨率裁剪，从而从UI交互视频中恢复可执行的状态转换，并生成对应的HTML/CSS/JavaScript代码，显著提升了功能正确性，特别是在多步密集交互场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型在处理UI视频生成网页代码时，通常采用稀疏采样或压缩时序表示，容易丢失短动作边界，破坏状态-动作-状态的转换链条，导致状态-转换对齐失败。

Method: Video2Code首先进行粗粒度视频理解定位动作关键区域，然后调用时序裁剪工具以更高时间分辨率重新审视这些区域，最终生成HTML/CSS/JavaScript代码。该方法通过动作对齐的视频-代码监督进行实例化。

Result: 实验表明Video2Code显著增强了开源模型在UI视频到代码生成任务上的能力，在功能正确性上优于直接视频观察方法，尤其在密集多步交互场景中提升明显。

Conclusion: 将UI视频到代码生成建模为可执行状态转换恢复问题，并引入动作感知的时序重采样策略，是解决状态-转换对齐失败的有效路径。

Abstract: UI videos provide a natural input for generating interactive webpages, as they capture both webpage appearance and action-triggered state transitions. However, directly applying video-capable vision-language models to this task remains insufficient. Existing models typically rely on sparse sampling or compressed temporal representations, which may miss short action boundaries and break the state-action-state transitions needed to implement webpage behavior. We formulate UI video-to-code generation as executable state-transition recovery from interaction videos, and identify this failure mode as state-transition misalignment. We introduce Video2Code, an action-aware video-to-code approach for recovering executable UI state transitions. Rather than allocating the visual budget uniformly across the video, Video2Code first performs coarse video understanding to locate action-critical regions, then invokes a temporal clipping tool to revisit these regions at higher temporal resolution before generating HTML/CSS/JavaScript code. We instantiate Video2Code with action-aligned video-code supervision and evaluate it under both visual and functional criteria. Experiments show that Video2Code substantially strengthens the underlying open-source model for UI video-to-code generation, improving functional correctness over direct video observation, especially on dense multi-step interactions.

</details>


### [24] [CDER-SME: A Cross-Device Event-RGB Micro-Expression Dataset under Multi-Level Stress Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20715)
*Jingting Li,Hui Sha,Su-Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDER-SME，一个跨设备Event-RGB微表情数据集，包含92名受试者、1,963个专家标注样本，采用多级压力诱导框架和硬件无关的对齐管道，验证了事件动态与RGB线索的互补性，为真实场景下的微表情识别提供了实用基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微表情识别基准多局限于实验室受控环境和刚性硬件耦合的传感方式，缺乏对真实场景中高时间敏感性和生态效度的支持，亟需一个跨设备、硬件无关的对齐基准。

Method: 构建CDER-SME跨设备Event-RGB数据集，采用认知和社会多级压力诱导框架激发自发性情绪泄露；设计硬件无关的对齐管道实现时间同步和地标引导的空间配准；数据集采用三层结构，包含92名受试者、1,963个专家标注样本（AU和情绪），含790个Event-RGB对和210个高保真对齐对；基于此建立可复现的多模态基线。

Result: 跨模态融合相较于单模态方法性能提升，验证了事件动态与RGB线索的互补性；无需同轴校准即可实现跨设备对齐。

Conclusion: CDER-SME为真实场景下可部署的跨设备Event-RGB微表情识别提供了实用基准，推动了情感智能在生态效度方向的发展。

Abstract: Micro-expression recognition (MER) in realistic scenarios demands high temporal sensitivity and ecological validity, yet existing benchmarks are largely constrained to laboratory-controlled settings and rigid hardware-coupled sensing. We introduce CDER-SME, a cross-device Event-RGB dataset collected under a multi-level stress induction framework (cognitive and social) to elicit spontaneous emotional leakage. To enable reproducible acquisition with independent, decoupled sensors, we provide a hardware-agnostic alignment pipeline for temporal synchronization and landmark-guided spatial registration. CDER-SME adopts a three-tier structure with 92 subjects and 1,963 expert-annotated samples (Action Units and emotions), including 790 Event-RGB pairs and 210 high-fidelity aligned pairs. We further report a reproducible multimodal baseline, where cross-modal fusion improves performance over single-modality counterparts, supporting the complementarity of event dynamics and RGB cues. By removing the need for coaxial calibration, CDER-SME offers a practical benchmark for cross-device alignment and deployable Event-RGB MER in real-world affective intelligence.

</details>


### [25] [MIRAGE: Stealthy Visual Prompt Injection for Vulnerability Detection in Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20717)
*Xuelong Dai,Jianyu Ma,Boyang Ma,Biwei Yan,Yijun Yang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE，一种在受限可信平台下（如广告位、赞助卡片等）针对多模态大语言模型网页代理的视觉间接提示注入攻击框架。该方法利用扩散模型生成感知无害的对抗性图像，并结合曲率感知对抗扩散引导与稀疏暗像素残差扰动优化，实现对SeeAct和OpenClaw等主流代理的下一步动作劫持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对MLLM网页代理的对抗性评估常依赖宽松的威胁模型和视觉上明显的伪影，缺乏在真实受限场景下的脆弱性检测方法。本文旨在探索一种更现实的攻击场景：评估者仅作为无特权的第三方（如商家或广告主），控制语义合法且空间受限的区域（如广告位、赞助卡片），实现对代理行为的定向劫持。

Method: 提出MIRAGE框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 利用扩散模型生成感知上良性的对抗性图像，严格限制在可信服务商允许的边界内；(2) 曲率感知对抗扩散引导（curvature-aware adversarial diffusion guidance）优化攻击效果；(3) 稀疏暗像素残差扰动（sparse, dark-pixel residual perturbations）增强攻击隐蔽性。在SeeAct和OpenClaw两个主流MLLM网页代理框架上进行评估。

Result: 在SeeAct和OpenClaw框架上的综合评估表明，MIRAGE在受限设置下实现了高效、逼真且隐蔽的视觉间接提示注入攻击，能够成功劫持代理的下一步动作。

Conclusion: MIRAGE揭示了MLLM网页代理在现实受限场景下的新攻击面，表明即使仅控制广告位等受限区域，第三方也能通过视觉对抗样本实现对代理行为的定向操控，凸显了当前多模态代理安全机制的不足。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based web agents provide practical, high-precision solutions for visual browser automation; however, they inherently expand the attack surface, introducing novel vision-based vulnerabilities. Existing adversarial evaluations targeting these agents frequently rely on permissive threat models and visually conspicuous artifacts. In this paper, we investigate a constrained vulnerability detection setting: a trusted web platform where the evaluator acts solely as an unprivileged third party, such as a merchant or advertiser, controlling only a semantically legitimate, spatially constrained region, such as an ad slot, a sponsored card, or a localized widget. Operating under these realistic constraints, we propose MIRAGE, a novel visual indirect prompt injection framework for targeted next-action hijacking. Our approach leverages diffusion models to generate perceptually benign adversarial images strictly confined to the attacker-controlled boundaries permitted by the trusted service provider. To maximize attack efficacy within such a restrictive setting, we introduce a robust optimization technique combining curvature-aware adversarial diffusion guidance with sparse, dark-pixel residual perturbations. Comprehensive evaluations against prominent MLLM web agent frameworks, specifically SeeAct and OpenClaw, empirically demonstrate the potency, realism, and stealth of our proposed MIRAGE.

</details>


### [26] [Evaluation of Medical Vision Language Models HuluMed and MedGemma, and general purpose chatbots Gemma 3, ChatGPT Plus, and Claude Pro on real previously unseen wound images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20723)
*Yunzhe Xue,Mohammed Saim Ahmed Quadri,Neal Panse,Justin W. Ady,Usman Roshan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了6种通用与医学专用视觉语言模型（VLM）在慢性伤口评估中的表现。使用20个临床多样化伤口案例和240个临床决策点，ChatGPT以72.50%正确率领先，Claude以62.08%紧随其后。开源医学专用模型表现较弱（HuluMed 40.00%最佳）。前沿通用多模态系统表现优于医学专用模型，但在高级伤口管理推理和临床自主可靠性方面仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性伤口评估在临床上具有挑战性，需要准确解读伤口形态、组织构成、血管特征和感染风险。尽管视觉语言模型为自动多模态伤口分析带来了可能，但现有模型在临床伤口评估中的实际表现缺乏系统评估。

Method: 使用20个涵盖血管、手术、缺血、静脉、淋巴水肿和截肢相关病因的临床多样化伤口案例，构建12个结构化临床问题框架（涵盖伤口分类、感染风险、血管干预建议、清创紧迫性、伤口治疗选择和高级管理规划）。评估6种VLM（ChatGPT、Claude、HuluMed、Gemma 3、MedGemma 4B、MedGemma 27B），与240个临床专家分级决策进行对比。

Result: ChatGPT表现最佳（174/240正确，72.50%），其次为Claude（149/240，62.08%）。开源医学专用模型中HuluMed最强（96/240，40.00%），其次是Gemma 3（81/240，33.75%）、MedGemma 4B（62/240，25.83%）、MedGemma 27B（42/240，17.50%）。

Conclusion: 前沿通用多模态系统在伤口分析方面表现显著优于医学专用模型，表明广泛的多模态推理能力与领域特定医学知识同等重要。当前VLM在临床决策支持方面具有潜力，但在高级伤口管理推理、程序规划和临床自主可靠性方面仍存在显著局限。

Abstract: Chronic wound assessment remains a clinically challenging task that requires accurate interpretation of wound morphology, tissue composition, vascular characteristics, and infection risk. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have introduced the possibility of automated multimodal wound analysis through image understanding combined with clinical reasoning. This study evaluates the performance of several general-purpose and medically specialized open-source and proprietary VLMs for clinical wound assessment using an expanded, curated dataset of 20 clinically diverse wounds spanning vascular, surgical, ischemic, venous, lymphedema, and amputation-related etiologies. Six VLMs were evaluated using a structured twelve-question clinical framework covering wound classification, infection risk, vascular intervention recommendations, debridement urgency, wound therapy selection, and advanced management planning. Across 20 wound cases and 240 clinician-graded wound-analysis decisions, ChatGPT achieved the highest overall performance with 174/240 correct responses (72.50%), followed by Claude with 149/240 (62.08%). Among the open-source and medically specialized models, HuluMed achieved the strongest performance with 96/240 correct responses (40.00%), followed by Gemma 3 (81/240, 33.75%), MedGemma 4B (62/240, 25.83%), and MedGemma 27B (42/240, 17.50%). The findings suggest that frontier general-purpose multimodal systems currently demonstrate substantially stronger wound-analysis performance than medically specialized alternatives, highlighting the continued importance of broad multimodal reasoning capabilities alongside domain-specific medical knowledge. Although current VLMs demonstrate promising potential for clinical decision support, substantial limitations remain in advanced wound-management reasoning, procedural planning, and autonomous clinical reliability.

</details>


### [27] [D2HDMap: Non-visible Driveline Map Prior for Online Vectorized HD Map Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20725)
*Seojun Shon,Chikao Tsuchiya,Dhaval Bhanderi,David Ilstrup,Hsinmin Cheng,Christopher Ostafew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D2HDMap，一种利用轻量级行驶轨迹先验（driveline prior）引导在线道路结构估计的建图系统，在无先验时仍保持良好性能，并在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶道路建图方法存在两难：高精地图成本高、维护难且过时后危及安全；纯传感器在线建图在远距离可靠性和遮挡处理上表现不佳。需要一种结合两者优势的轻量级先验方法。

Method: 提出D2HDMap，将轻量级、不可见（非视觉）的行驶轨迹先验（driveline prior）注入在线建图系统，引导车道分隔线、道路边界和人行横道等可见道路结构的估计。该先验创建和更新成本远低于完整高精地图先验。同时采用噪声感知训练增强对定位误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上的消融实验表明，使用行驶轨迹先验训练的模型即使在先验不可用时也能基本保持性能。在地理分离的数据划分上，D2HDMap达到44.8 mAP，超越最新方法。噪声感知训练显著提升对实际定位误差的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: D2HDMap通过轻量级行驶轨迹先验有效平衡了高精地图与纯在线建图的优劣，在降低维护成本的同时提升了在线建图的准确性和泛化能力，且具备对定位噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate, up-to-date representations of road structures are critical for the safe operation of autonomous vehicles. Existing systems rely either on costly, maintenance-heavy high-definition (HD) maps which compromise safety when outdated, or purely sensor-based online mapping which struggles with long-range reliability and occlusion. Systems incorporating map prior information into online mapping seek to overcome drawbacks of both approaches by combining them in some way. We propose 'Driveline To HD Map' (D2HDMap), an online mapping system that injects a lightweight, non-visible driveline prior to guide the estimation of visible road structures such as lane dividers, road boundaries and crosswalks. This prior incurs less effort to create and update compared to full HD map priors used in other approaches. We also show that training with such a prior can improve generalization at inference time when no prior is available. Ablation studies conducted on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 dataset demonstrate that models trained using a driveline prior largely retain performance even when priors are not available. On a geographically disjoint split, D2HDMap achieves 44.8 mAP, surpassing recent state-of-the-art. Additionally, noise-aware training substantially increases robustness to realistic localization error.

</details>


### [28] [How Well Can Your Video Model Remember? Measuring Memory-Budget Trade-offs in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20726)
*Yixian Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量化长视频理解中回答准确率与帧预算B和时间距离D关系的经验模型，发现logit准确率与log预算呈线性关系，其斜率（预算指数α(D)）随距离对数线性衰减。基于约15.5万次二元预测拟合模型，揭示了不同模型和采样策略下的性能退化规律，并将α(D)作为流式视频模型的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频模型在严格预算下运行，但现有研究缺乏一个能够预测准确率如何随帧预算缩减和时间距离增大而退化的理论框架。

Method: 对10个模型和3种采样策略的约15.5万个二元预测进行加权最小二乘拟合，建立logit准确率与log预算的线性关系，其中预算指数α(D)随距离对数线性衰减。

Result: 模型在细胞级加权R²=0.05-0.75范围内有效。STREAMINGVLM在D=1000s时α=1.26，10倍预算增加带来+29个百分点的准确率提升；而Qwen3-VL基础模型α=0.17，仅提升+4个百分点。预算有效性在同类最佳模型间相差约7.4倍。

Conclusion: α(D)可作为流式视频模型的诊断指标，指导原理性预算分配，并揭示了长距离下的模型排名反转现象。

Abstract: We introduce a compact empirical model that quantifies how answer accuracy degrades as a function of frame budget B and temporal distance D in long video understanding -- analyzing performance when recalling content from D seconds in the past using a fraction B of total frames. Long-form models operate under strict budgets, yet no prior framework predicts how accuracy degrades as B shrinks and events recede. We fit a weighted least-squares model on ~155,000 binary predictions across ten models and three sampling strategies, deriving a law where logit-accuracy scales linearly in log-budget with a distance-dependent exponent that decays log-linearly with distance. This budget exponent α(D) captures the marginal value of extra frames at distance D. The law achieves cell-level weighted R^2 = 0.05-0.75 across models. Notably, budget effectiveness at D = 1000 s differs by \approx 7.4\times between the best streaming and base models. STREAMINGVLM achieves α(1000) = 1.26 (95% CI: [1.06, 1.58]), meaning a tenfold budget increase substantially improves long-distance accuracy, while the best Qwen3-VL base model reaches only α(1000) = 0.17 (CI: [0.04, 0.34]). In accuracy space, a 10\times budget increase at D = 1000 s yields +29 percentage points for STREAMINGVLM versus +4 pp for the base model. Sampling strategies show model-dependent trade-offs: random sampling yields higher base sensitivity but steeper distance decay. We demonstrate how α(D) enables principled budget allocation, including a model-ranking reversal at long distance, and propose it as a diagnostic metric for streaming video models.

</details>


### [29] [VTOS: Learning to Orchestrate Vision Tools by Co-Searching Solutions and Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20728)
*Jinchao Ge,Lingqiao Liu,Shuwen Zhao,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTOS是一个通过联合搜索解决方案程序与观察者程序来自适应编排视觉工具（如Grounding DINO、SAM、NMS等）的框架，利用共享的VisionThoughts知识库积累观察反馈以指导后续搜索，在密集目标计数和零样本植物病害分割任务上优于静态工具流水线和智能体式视觉编程基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉编程智能体通常生成固定的解决方案流水线，在密集目标、遮挡、小目标和域偏移等场景下表现脆弱，缺乏根据视觉条件自适应调整工具编排的能力。

Method: VTOS框架联合搜索两类程序：可执行的解决方案程序（组合各类视觉工具）和观察者程序（诊断候选方案的失败模式并生成可操作反馈），并将观察结果累积在共享的VisionThoughts知识库中指导后续搜索。

Result: 在LVIS-Count密集目标计数和PlantSeg-OOD零样本植物病害分割两个案例研究中，VTOS均优于静态工具流水线和智能体式视觉编程基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合搜索解决方案和观察者是一种有效的策略，能够自适应地将视觉工具编排以应对具有挑战性的计算机视觉任务。

Abstract: Vision foundation tools such as open-vocabulary detectors, segmentation models, and post-processing operators are powerful building blocks for computer vision, but their effectiveness depends heavily on how they are orchestrated: which tools are used, in what order, with what parameters, and under what visual conditions. Existing visual-programming agents typically generate a fixed solution pipeline, making them brittle under dense objects, occlusion, small targets, and domain shift. We introduce VTOS (Vision Tools Orchestration Search), a framework for adaptive visual tool orchestration through joint solution--observer search. VTOS co-searches executable solution programs that compose vision tools such as Grounding DINO, SAM, NMS, and slice-and-detect, together with observer programs that diagnose candidate solutions, identify failure modes, and generate actionable feedback. These observations are accumulated in a shared VisionThoughts knowledge base to guide subsequent search. We evaluate VTOS through two case studies: dense object counting on LVIS-Count and zero-shot plant-disease segmentation on PlantSeg-OOD, which stress different orchestration challenges including threshold calibration, NMS, slicing, mask refinement, and domain generalization. Across both tasks, VTOS outperforms static tool pipelines and agentic visual-programming baselines, showing that co-searching solutions and observers is an effective strategy for adapting vision tools to challenging computer vision tasks.

</details>


### [30] [XmoPipe: A Pipeline for Large-Scale In-the-Wild Human Motion Dataset Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20731)
*Nathan Salazar,Emmanuel Dellandréa,Mathieu Lefort,Alexandre Meyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可扩展的流水线，通过从在线视频中自动提取3D人体和面部运动并生成文本描述，来构建大规模野外人体运动数据集，替代昂贵的传统动作捕捉方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于标记的动作捕捉数据成本高昂、规模有限且多样性不足，而网络视频资源丰富但缺乏有效的运动提取手段，因此需要一种可扩展的流水线来从网络视频中自动构建大规模、多样化的野外人体运动数据集。

Method: 提出的流水线从关键词出发，自动检索相关视频，从中提取3D人体和面部运动数据，并生成高级文本描述。该流水线灵活可扩展，支持针对不同动作类型、多人交互或表达性行为的定向数据收集。

Result: 通过训练运动重建和运动生成模型，结果表明该方法训练出的模型性能与传统动作捕捉数据集上训练的模型相当，且展现出强大的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了从网络视频自动构建高质量人体运动数据集的可行性，为大规模、多样化的运动模型训练提供了低成本、可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Large-scale human motion datasets are essential for training robust motion models for analysis, synthesis, and understanding. While marker-based motion capture provides precise data, it is costly and limited in scale and diversity. Recent advances in monocular motion capture and video-language understanding open the way to extract plausible motion from unconstrained online videos. We present a scalable pipeline for constructing in-the-wild human motion datasets. From a few keywords, the system retrieves videos, extracts 3D body and facial motion, and generates high-level textual descriptions. The pipeline is flexible, enabling targeted collection of various motions, multi-person interactions, or expressive behaviors. We demonstrate its quality by training motion reconstruction and motion generation models, showing performance comparable to models trained on traditional motion capture datasets and strong cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [31] [Robust Zero-Shot Generalization for Open-Vocabulary Action Recognition via Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20734)
*Francesca Morandi,Omayma Moussadek,Federico Venturini,Mauro Suardi,Alessandro Banzatti,Francesco Cannarile,Angelo Porrello,Simone Calderara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需目标域微调的开放词汇动作识别（OVAR）新范式，通过模型合并和任务算术从多个公开数据集提取并组合任务向量，在分布外场景下实现了优于预训练基模型的零样本泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的开放词汇动作识别在真实场景中通常需要领域特定的微调，这成本高昂且引发隐私和监管问题。作者旨在探索一种绕过目标域训练的替代范式，通过重组现有数据集和模型的知识来提升零样本泛化能力。

Method: 利用模型合并和任务算术技术，从在不同公开OVAR数据集上微调的模型中提取任务向量，并将这些任务向量进行组合，得到一个融合模型，无需在目标域上进行任何训练。

Result: 在分布外（out-of-distribution）设置下，合并后的模型相比预训练的基模型取得了更优的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过模型合并和任务向量组合，可以在无需目标域微调的情况下提升OVAR模型的泛化能力，为实际部署中降低成本和隐私风险提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Open Vocabulary Action Recognition (OVAR) enables the recognition of novel actions by leveraging vision-language representations, overcoming the limitations of traditional closed-set approaches. However, achieving robust performance in real-world scenarios typically requires domain-specific fine-tuning, which is often costly and raises privacy and regulatory concerns. In this work, we propose an alternative paradigm that bypasses target-domain training and recombines knowledge from existing datasets and models. Leveraging model merging and task arithmetic, we extract and combine task vectors from models fine-tuned on diverse public OVAR datasets. We show that, in out-of-distribution settings, the resulting merged model achieves superior zero-shot generalization to the pre-trained base model. Code is available at https://github.com/omaymaMoussadek/robust-ovar

</details>


### [32] [REKEY: Metadata-Grounded Visual-Key Regeneration for Contamination-Resilient VQA Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20736)
*Tengjie Lin,Yutao Sun,Jingwei Ni,Shuhan Ge,Hao-Xuan Ma,Yanting Miao,Wangyue Lu,Mingshuai Chen,Tiancheng Zhao,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReKey 是一种实时基准测试协议，通过在评估时随机重新生成图像中的"视觉键"（答案相关的局部细节），防止 VQA 基准测试因数据泄露而失效。在 V*Bench 上应用后，八个前沿 VLM 在原始项上的得分比再生变体高出 9.5-18.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态 VQA 基准测试一旦数据泄露到训练语料中，分数反映的是记忆而非真正的视觉能力，而人工重建高质量基准成本高昂且新版本很快又会泄露。

Method: 提出 ReKey 协议，在评估时对真实图像中经人工验证的编辑槽随机重新生成携带答案的局部细节（视觉键），从而生成带有新答案、基于构建的标签和受控视觉搜索难度的新实例。

Result: 在 V*Bench 上，8 个前沿 VLM 在原始项上的得分比 ReKey 再生变体高出 9.5-18.8 个百分点，表明原始基准存在显著的记忆效应。

Conclusion: 通过使视觉键可更新，ReKey 能够在模型和训练数据演进过程中持续保持评估的新鲜度和有效性，为 VQA 评估提供了一种对抗数据泄露的可持续方案。

Abstract: Static visual question answering (VQA) benchmarks age quickly: Once the items leak into training corpora, scores can reflect memorization rather than genuine visual ability, thus obscuring real progress. Rebuilding high-quality benchmarks such as V*Bench requires substantial human annotation, yet each static release can quickly become another leaked artifact. We propose ReKey, a live benchmark protocol that randomly regenerates the answer-bearing local detail, or visual key, in real images at evaluation time. Using human-validated edit slots, ReKey samples fresh instances with new answers, construction-grounded labels, and controlled visual-search difficulty. On V*Bench, the ReKey regenerated benchmark reveals a sharp score jump across eight frontier vision-language models (VLMs): The original items score 9.5--18.8 percentage points higher than the regenerated variants. By making the visual key renewable, ReKey keeps evaluation fresh as models and training data evolve.

</details>


### [33] [An approach with Visual and Tabular Mamba to multimodal medical data using Mixed Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20738)
*Matheus B. Rocha,Gustavo B. Dettogni,Renato A. Krohling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mamba架构（状态空间模型）的混合多模态融合方法，用于医学数据中的癌症分类。该方法包含两个Mamba变体：一个处理视觉数据（病变图像分类），另一个处理表格数据（结合临床/社会人口学信息进行最终诊断）。在PAD-UFES-20（皮肤病变）和NDB-UFES（口腔癌）数据集上的实验表明，该方法在平衡准确率上略低于Transformer方法（PAD-UFES-20），但在NDB-UFES上表现更优，且在召回率指标上有显著提升。采用Mixed Fusion架构还支持SHAP可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的多模态医学数据融合方法虽然性能优异，但存在计算复杂度高、可解释性不足等问题。作者希望探索基于状态空间模型（Mamba）的轻量级替代方案，在保持分类性能的同时提升模型的可解释性和对敏感类别的召回率。

Method: 提出Mixed Fusion混合多模态融合架构，包含两个Mamba变体：视觉Mamba处理病变/组织病理图像并生成类别概率，表格Mamba将这些概率与临床/社会人口学数据结合进行最终诊断。该架构支持后续使用SHAP方法增强可解释性。

Result: 在PAD-UFES-20数据集上平衡准确率略低于Transformer方法，在NDB-UFES数据集上表现更优；在召回率指标上有显著提升；Mixed Fusion架构支持SHAP可解释性分析。

Conclusion: 基于Mamba的模型可作为医学数据多模态分类的合适替代方案，尤其适用于对敏感性（召回率）有较高要求的场景。

Abstract: This article presents a complementary approach for integrating multimodal medical data in cancer classification, based on state space models represented by the Mamba architecture. To this end, a mixed multimodal fusion architecture, called Mixed Fusion, was employed and developed to enhance the interpretability of the decision-making process. The proposed approach explores two variants of Mamba: one dedicated to visual processing, responsible for classifying the lesion image and generating probabilities associated with the target classes, and another focused on tabular processing, which uses these probabilities together with clinical and/or sociodemographic data to produce the final diagnosis. The experiments were conducted on two medical datasets: PAD-UFES-20, composed of clinical images and information associated with skin lesions, and NDB-UFES, consisting of histopathological images and sociodemographic data related to oral cancer. The results indicate slightly lower performance in balanced accuracy, compared with Transformer-based approaches, on PAD-UFES-20, and superior performance on NDB-UFES. Additionally, substantial gains were observed in the recall metric. Furthermore, the adoption of the Mixed Fusion architecture enables the application of the Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) method, increasing the interpretability of the results. These findings indicate that Mamba-based models constitute a suitable alternative for multimodal classification in medical data, especially in scenarios in which sensitivity is a relevant requirement.

</details>


### [34] [Mirage: a Clean-Label Backdoor against LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20752)
*Ziba Parsons,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mirage是一种针对基于深度神经网络的LiDAR 3D目标检测的黑盒、干净标签后门攻击方法，仅需0.5%的投毒率即可达到73%的误分类成功率，且保持模型正常性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击通常需要白盒访问或标签修改，且集中于几何攻击（如物体消失或边界框操纵），缺乏黑盒且保持标签一致性的攻击方案。

Method: Mirage向训练集中注入少量标签一致的投毒样本，使模型学习触发器模式与目标类别之间的恶意关联，同时保留正常训练语义，实现黑盒、干净标签攻击。

Result: 在多个SOTA LiDAR 3DOD模型和基准数据集上，Mirage以0.5%的投毒率实现了73%的误分类成功率，且检测性能与良性模型几乎一致。

Conclusion: Mirage证明了LiDAR 3D目标检测系统在缺乏白盒访问和标签修改的情况下仍面临严重后门安全威胁，需引起重视。

Abstract: Deep neural network-based LiDAR 3D object detection serves as a critical perception component in safety-critical autonomous systems. However, recent studies have revealed its vulnerability to backdoor attacks. Existing attacks typically require white-box access or label modification and focus on geometric attacks such as object disappearance or bounding-box manipulation. In this paper, we present Mirage, a black-box and clean-label backdoor attack against deep neural network-based LiDAR 3DOD. Mirage injects a small number of label-consistent poisoning samples into the training set, causing the model to learn a malicious association between a trigger pattern and an attacker-chosen target class while preserving normal training semantics. As a result, the compromised model behaves normally on benign inputs yet systematically misclassifies triggered objects as the target class during deployment. We evaluate Mirage on multiple state-of-the-art LiDAR 3DOD models and benchmark datasets. Experimental results show that Mirage achieves a 73% misclassification success rate with a poisoning rate of only 0.5%, while maintaining detection performance close to that of benign models.

</details>


### [35] [UniSLAD: A Unified Framework for Structural and Logical Industrial Visual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20768)
*Changyi Li,Chao Yang,Yu Xiao,Kari Tammi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一框架UniSLAD，无需额外训练即可同时检测结构异常和逻辑异常，通过双特征提取器（CNN+Transformer）和双粒度特征表示模块，在工业基准上取得99.4%和93.1%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉异常检测方法在结构缺陷检测上取得进展，但逻辑异常检测研究不足，且两者在实际工业流程中常同时出现，缺乏能同时检测两者的统一解决方案。

Method: 提出UniSLAD框架：1）双特征提取器，融合CNN骨干（局部纹理感知）与Transformer骨干（全局上下文推理）；2）双粒度特征表示模块，在patch级利用马氏变换增强的记忆库进行异常评分，在图像级通过LUM和PMP聚合分布图获得鲁棒全局表示。

Result: 在两个工业基准数据集上分别达到99.4%和93.1%的竞争性能，消融实验验证了各组件有效性。

Conclusion: UniSLAD无需额外训练即可同时检测逻辑异常和结构异常，为动态工业环境提供了实用解决方案，推动了综合异常检测研究。

Abstract: Visual anomaly detection is a fundamental task in industrial automation. While existing approaches have achieved notable progress in identifying structural defects, the detection of logical anomalies remains relatively underexplored. In practice, structural and logical anomalies frequently co-occur in industrial workflows. Therefore, a solution capable of detecting both structural and logical anomalies is crucial for advancing comprehensive anomaly detection research. To address this limitation, we propose a unified framework, termed UniSLAD, which jointly addresses logical and structural anomalies without additional training, enabling a practical solution for dynamic industrial environments. First, we introduce a dual-feature extractor that synergistically integrates a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) backbone for local texture perception with a Transformer backbone for global contextual reasoning, yielding richer and more comprehensive representations. Building on this foundation, we design dual-granularity feature representation modules. At the patch level, memory banks enhanced by the Mahalanobis Transform (MT) preserve representative features and support more discriminative anomaly scoring. At the image level, distribution maps are aggregated using Lower-Upper Mean (LUM) and Power Mean Pooling (PMP), yielding a more robust global representation than conventional average pooling. Extensive experiments on the two industrial benchmarks demonstrate that UniSLAD achieves competitive performance in comprehensive anomaly detection, achieving 99.4% and 93.1%, respectively. Furthermore, ablation studies verify the individual contributions and effectiveness of each proposed component.

</details>


### [36] [TriMotion: Modality-Agnostic Camera Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20774)
*Seunghyun Shin,Jifei Song,Wooseok Jeon,Hae-Gon Jeon,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriMotion是一个跨模态统一的相机控制视频生成框架，通过构建共享的运动嵌入空间，支持视频、姿态轨迹和文本三种模态的输入，并引入潜在运动一致性目标实现高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机运动控制方法通常依赖单一模态（如显式姿态轨迹或参考视频）来条件化生成过程，无法支持异构用户输入，限制了灵活性和实用性。

Method: 1) 构建Motion Triplet数据集，将多相机视频数据集扩展为包含视频、姿态和文本描述的三元组；2) 设计模态无关框架，将三种模态映射到共享运动嵌入空间；3) 引入潜在运动一致性目标，在潜在空间直接约束生成视频跟随目标相机轨迹，避免像素空间解码开销。

Result: TriMotion在三种模态输入下均能生成高质量视频，准确跟随目标相机轨迹，并支持顺序运动组合和跨模态运动插值等灵活应用。

Conclusion: 共享运动嵌入空间有效统一了异构输入，实现了模态无关的相机控制视频生成，且具备良好的泛化性和扩展能力。

Abstract: Camera motion control is essential for directing viewpoint changes in generative systems. However, existing methods typically condition the generation process on a single specific modality, such as explicit pose trajectories or reference videos, limiting their ability to support heterogeneous user inputs. To address this limitation, we present TriMotion, a modality-agnostic framework for camera-controlled video generation that maps video, pose, and text inputs, describing the same camera trajectory into a shared motion embedding space. Learning such a space requires synchronized supervision across modalities. Therefore, we build the Motion Triplet Dataset by extending a Multi-Cam Video Dataset with geometry-grounded motion descriptions derived from camera extrinsics. We further introduce a latent motion consistency objective that leverages the motion embedding space to encourage the generated video to follow the target camera trajectory directly in latent space, avoiding the cost of pixel-space decoding. Extensive experiments show that TriMotion generates high-quality videos that accurately follow the target camera trajectories across all three modalities. Beyond standard generation, the shared motion embedding space also enables flexible applications such as sequential motion composition and cross-modal motion interpolation.

</details>


### [37] [GroundShot: Visually Consistent Multi-Shot Long Video Generation via Entity-Grounded Shot Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20799)
*Yixuan Lai,Tianjia Shao,Kun Zhou,Weijia Dou,Siyu Zhu,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GroundShot是一个无需训练、模型无关的智能体框架，通过构建实体级视觉记忆在线管理多镜头视频生成中的一致性；GroundBench是用于诊断性评估实体级一致性的基准。实验表明GroundShot在不需额外训练或模型修改的情况下提升了多镜头一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多镜头视频生成中，随着镜头数量增加，跨镜头重新出现的实体（角色、物体、地点）会出现外观漂移，导致视觉不一致。现有的方法缺乏对跨镜头实体一致性的系统管理。

Method: 提出了GroundShot框架，包含四个核心组件：1) 按实体参考价值调度镜头生成顺序；2) 从生成视频中提取实体；3) 验证实体可靠性后再加入视觉记忆；4) 在生成每个镜头前从记忆中检索合适的实体参考。

Result: GroundShot在无需训练或模型修改的条件下，相比现有方法显著提升了多镜头视频生成的一致性。

Conclusion: 通过构建并在线维护实体级视觉记忆，可以有效解决多镜头视频生成中的跨镜头实体一致性漂移问题，且该方法具有训练免费和模型无关的实用优势。

Abstract: Generating visually consistent multi-shot videos remains an open challenge. As videos span more shots, inconsistencies can accumulate across shots, causing entities that reappear across shots -- characters, objects, and locations -- to drift away from how they first appear. We observe that viewers judge consistency by comparing each later appearance of an entity with its first clear appearance; the visual quality of this initial appearance sets the consistency ceiling for all that follows. Motivated by this, we present \textbf{GroundShot}, a training-free, model-agnostic agentic framework for entity-grounded multi-shot generation. GroundShot builds an entity-level visual memory online from accepted generated shots: it schedules shots' generation order by their expected usefulness as entity references, grounds entities from generated videos, verifies their reliability before adding them to memory, and retrieves suitable entity references from memory before each shot is generated. To evaluate this entity-centered view of consistency, we further introduce \textbf{GroundBench}, a diagnostic benchmark that measures consistency at the entity level while isolating controlled challenge dimensions. Experiments show that GroundShot improves multi-shot consistency over existing methods while requiring no additional training or model modification.

</details>


### [38] [NeoLoc-68: End-to-end 68-point neonatal facial landmark localisation in neonatal clinical environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20823)
*Abdullah Bin-Obaid,Maria M. Cobo,Rebeccah Slater,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端68点新生儿面部关键点检测模型，结合11个公开数据集（37,459张图像）与新生儿临床数据集（1,123帧），基于YOLO关键点模型改进，在公开数据集上达到SOTA性能（NME=5.37, FR=12.5%），在临床新生儿测试集上经微调后DFR降至1.77%，为新生儿疼痛评估等下游任务奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部关键点检测器在成人面孔上表现良好，但在新生儿临床环境中因医疗设备遮挡、头部姿态多变、运动模糊等问题性能严重下降，缺乏专门针对新生儿面部关键点检测的方法，阻碍了自动化、非接触式新生儿疼痛评估技术的发展。

Method: 收集并标准化11个公开数据集（37,459张单人脸图像）和1个新生儿研究数据集（1,123帧手动标注），统一为68点标注格式；采用YOLO-based关键点模型进行面部关键点回归，并使用预训练的新生儿面部检测器权重进行模型初始化。

Result: 公开数据集：NME=5.37, FR=12.5%, AUC0.08=38.00%, AUC0.1=48.70%；临床新生儿测试集微调前DFR=5.3%为所有基线最低，微调后NME=6.36, FR=22.30%, DFR=1.77%, AUC0.08=29.24%, AUC0.1=40.25%。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了端到端68点新生儿面部关键点检测，在公开和临床数据集上均取得优异性能，通过进一步数据集扩展和优化，可支持新生儿健康监测和疼痛相关面部分析等下游任务。

Abstract: Facial landmark localisation is a prerequisite for developing automated, non-contact neonatal pain assessment methods. Clinicians use pain scales to judge the severity of pain, many of which rely on facial expression. However, facial landmark detectors trained on adult faces perform poorly in neonatal clinical environments due to frequent occlusions caused by medical equipment, varied head poses, and challenging imaging conditions, including motion blur triggered by sudden pain-related movements. We propose an end-to-end facial landmark detector capable of predicting 68 landmarks on neonatal faces in clinical environments. We combined 37,459 single-face images from 11 public datasets, standardised to 68-point markup, with 1,123 manually annotated frames from a neonatal research dataset (totalling over 76,000 landmarks). A YOLO-based keypoint model was adapted to regress the facial landmarks, initialised with weights from a pretrained neonatal face detector. On public datasets, our proposed model achieved state-of-the-art performance: Normalised Mean Error (NME) = 5.37, Failure Rate (FR) = 12.5%, Area Under the Cumulative Error Curve (AUC) at AUC0.08 = 38.00% and AUC0.1 = 48.70%. On the clinical neonatal test set, before fine-tuning, the model achieved the lowest Detection Failure Rate (DFR) = 5.3% among all baselines and showed strong generalisation. After fine-tuning, performance improved further to NME = 6.36, FR = 22.30%, DFR = 1.77%, AUC0.08 = 29.24% and AUC0.1 = 40.25%. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first end-to-end 68-point neonatal facial landmark detection model. With further dataset expansion and refinement, it could support downstream tasks in neonatal health monitoring and pain-related facial analysis.

</details>


### [39] [From Uncertainty to Stability and Fidelity: Guiding Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting with Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20842)
*Junbao Zhou,Qingshan Xu,Yuan Zhou,Xiaolong Shen,Beier Zhu,Kesen Zhao,Yiming Zeng,Chen Bai,Cheng Lu,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher Information的稀疏视角3D Gaussian Splatting方法，通过量化引导几何先验利用和正则化（包括选择性立体增强和不确定性感知正则化），有效缓解过拟合，在稀疏视角新视角合成中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视角3DGS方法存在两个问题：(1) 基于先验的数据增强随机选取新视角，缺乏选择依据；(2) Dropout正则化随机移除高斯体，引入不确定性。这些随机性导致优化不稳定、渲染质量受限，且缺乏原则性指导。

Method: 方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于Fisher Information的立体增强——主动选择信息量最丰富的支撑视角，利用深度先验生成可靠的伪真实值，减少增强中的随机性；(2) 不确定性感知正则化——利用Fisher Information量化每个3D高斯体的不确定性，自适应调整移除概率，提高Dropout正则化的稳定性和有效性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在稀疏视角新视角合成基准测试中取得了最先进的性能，有效缓解了过拟合，提升了优化稳定性和渲染保真度。

Conclusion: 通过Fisher Information量化引导先验增强和正则化，能够显著提升稀疏视角下3DGS的渲染质量和优化稳定性，为稀疏视角神经渲染提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising technique for novel view synthesis. However, 3DGS requires dense input views to achieve high-quality rendering. In sparse-view scenarios, 3DGS often prones to overfitting, resulting in noticeable artifacts and degraded rendering quality. Previous methods explore to address this issue by introducing additional priors (e.g. depth priors) or integrating regularization techniques (e.g. Dropout). However, these methods are often applied without principled guidance. In particular, prior-based augmentation typically samples novel viewpoints randomly, while Dropout-based regularization randomly removes Gaussians. The compounded randomness introduces uncertainty and instability, limiting the fidelity of novel view synthesis. In this paper, we propose a novel method for sparse-view 3DGS that incorporates Fisher Information to quantitatively guide the utilization of geometric priors and regularization. Specifically, our method comprises two key components: (1) Stereo augmentation with Fisher Information. By leveraging Fisher Information, we actively select most informative supporting views and use depth priors to curate reliable pseudo ground truths, which reduces randomness in augmentation and improves stability and rendering fidelity; (2) Uncertainty-aware regularization. We reduce the instability of Dropout-based regularization by using Fisher Information to quantitatively measure the uncertainty of each 3D Gaussian, and adaptively adjust the removal probability, leading to more stable and effective regularization. With these two components, our method effectively mitigates overfitting and improves the stability of optimization in sparse-view 3DGS, resulting in superior rendering fidelity. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in sparse-view novel view synthesis benchmarks.

</details>


### [40] [Translating Inference-Time Control to Radiology Vision-Language Models: Activation Steering for Pneumonia Classification on Chest X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20852)
*Eduardo Moreno Judice de Mattos Farina,Mateus A. Esmeraldo,Felipe Akio Matsuoka,Paulo Eduardo de Aguiar Kuriki,Felipe Campos Kitamura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了对比激活添加（CAA）能否在不微调的情况下提升胸部X光片视觉语言模型（VLM）的肺炎分类性能。在三个冻结的VLM上测试发现，CAA能显著改变预测分数分布，但仅在NV-Reason-CXR-3B上观察到有意义的性能提升（F1从0.7692提升至0.8727），表明激活引导可作为轻量级适配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需微调的推理时工程方法（CAA）是否能提升医学视觉语言模型（VLM）的诊断性能，为模型适配提供轻量级替代方案。

Method: 在三个冻结的胸部X光VLM（MedGemma-4B-IT, NV-Reason-CXR-3B, CheXOne-3B）上，基于二元提示的Yes/No token logits进行肺炎分类。使用三类引导向量（答案偏置控制、肺炎文本对比、图像条件对比），通过200张确定性图像集进行层/尺度选择和阈值校准，并采用ROC-AUC、PR-AUC、F1分数、阈值分析、反向/随机向量控制及条件自助置信区间进行性能评估。

Result: CAA显著改变预测分数分布和操作特性。NV-Reason-CXR-3B受益最大（F1从0.7692提升至0.8619/0.8727）；CheXOne-3B有微弱提升（0.8528→0.8666，置信区间跨零）；MedGemma-4B-IT改进不持续。固定阈值F1改进频繁出现但未一致表明诊断性能提升。

Conclusion: CAA可作为一种无需微调的轻量级医学VLM行为适配方法，但在三个模型中仅有一个获得有意义的性能提升，其通用有效性需进一步验证。

Abstract: Inference-time engineering can alter model behavior without fine-tuning. However, its utility for improving diagnostic performance in medical vision-language models (VLMs) remains unclear. We aim to evaluate whether Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) can improve pneumonia classification in chest radiograph VLMs without updating model weights. Three frozen chest radiograph VLMs (MedGemma-4B-IT, NV-Reason-CXR-3B, and CheXOne-3B) were evaluated on the public Kermany pneumonia test set. Classification was based on the logits of the tokens Yes and No under a binary prompt. Steering vectors included a 30-pair answer-bias control, a 30-pair pneumonia text contrast, and an image-conditioned contrast derived from 30 pneumonia and 30 normal development images. A deterministic 200-image development set was used for layer and scale selection (100 images) and threshold calibration (100 images). Performance was assessed using ROC-AUC, PR-AUC, F1 score, threshold analyses, reverse-vector controls, random-vector controls, and conditional bootstrap confidence intervals. Fixed-threshold F1 improvements were frequently observed but did not consistently indicate improved diagnostic performance. For MedGemma-4B-IT. NV-Reason-CXR-3B showed the strongest benefit: calibrated F1 improved from 0.7692 in the zero-shot setting to 0.8619 with pneumonia-text steering and to 0.8727 with image-conditioned steering. For CheXOne-3B, pneumonia-text steering increased calibrated F1 from 0.8528 to 0.8666, although the confidence interval crossed zero. On this public pneumonia benchmark, CAA substantially altered prediction score distributions and operating characteristics without fine-tuning. Meaningful performance gains were observed in one of three evaluated VLMs, suggesting that activation steering may serve as a lightweight approach for adapting medical VLM behavior.

TL;DR: FOCA是一种面向未来的条件框架，通过显式预测任务相关的未来交互嵌入和隐式对齐未来目标观测，显著提升了VLA模型在少样本模仿学习中的性能，在多个基准和真实机器人上实现了最先进的适应效果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在少样本模仿学习场景下性能急剧下降，现有适应策略存在关键弱点，亟需数据高效的适应方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable general-purpose robotic control via large-scale multimodal pretraining, yet their effectiveness under few-shot imitation learning remains limited. We conduct a systematic stress test of state-of-the-art VLA models and show that performance degrades sharply as demonstrations are reduced, revealing a key weakness of existing adaptation strategies. To address this, we introduce FOCA, a future-oriented conditioning framework for data-efficient VLA adaptation. FOCA combines explicit prediction of task-grounded future interaction embeddings with implicit alignment to future goal observations, enabling long-horizon reasoning in latent space without pixel-level prediction. This formulation naturally supports action-free co-training with synthetic videos from video world models and can be interpreted as learning a future-conditioned value-like representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate FOCA achieves 95.7% success with 20 demonstrations on LIBERO, improves 7-12% on RoboCasa, and delivers up to 26% absolute gains on real robots, establishing a new state of the art in few-shot VLA adaptation.

</details>

Abstract: As urban areas expand, automatic monitoring of parking lots becomes essential for efficient and sustainable cities. This work proposes a self-supervised approach for parking spot occupancy recognition that requires no labeled samples from the target parking lot. Building upon a self-supervised transfer learning fine-tuning protocol, the proposed training strategy consists of two self-supervised stages: first on unlabeled generic data and then on unlabeled target-specific data, followed by supervised fine-tuning using only generic parking lot labels. We adopt SimCLR with a ResNet-50 encoder and evaluate the method under a leave-one-out cross-environment protocol on three public datasets: PKLot, CNRPark-EXT, and PLds. We also introduce a two-stage deployment strategy in which a Strong General Model is initially deployed, followed by a Specialized Model that incorporates unlabeled images collected during the first N days of deployment in a self-supervised manner. Experimental results show that the Strong General Model alone outperforms supervised and self-supervised baselines, achieving an average accuracy of 97.2%, which further improves to 97.8% with the proposed two-stage strategy. These results demonstrate that self-supervised learning enables a scalable and labelefficient solution for real-world parking occupancy monitoring. Our trained models and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/LoanMaikon/Parking-Spot-Occupancy-Recognition.

Abstract: In this work, we propose \methodname, an LLM-based model for fine-grained human motion understanding that represents motion as a sequence of skeletal poses with explicit timestamps for each pose. Each pose encodes body joint positions and is temporally grounded with timestamp tokens, allowing the model to reason about motion order, duration, and rhythm. To study what supervision is needed for motion-language reasoning, we construct a diverse training mixture spanning pose captioning, pose question answering, motion captioning, and motion question answering. Our ablations show that the primary gains come from the diversity of pose- and motion-level supervision, while staged training provides a smaller additional benefit. Different from previous works that rely on ground-truth 3D motion capture, our approach supports both 2D and 3D skeletal motion representations through a unified pose encoder, and can optionally incorporate video to provide contextual information. Extensive experiments on BABEL-QA, HuMMan-QA, CompMo, NTU-RGB+D, and QEVD-Coach demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit temporal encoding and diverse pose- and motion-level supervision for fine-grained human motion understanding. Notably, even when using only 2D skeletal input, our approach surpasses previous 3D-based methods.

Conclusion: 通过统一运动控制与参考合成，Go-with-the-Track成功弥合了现有方法的鸿沟，为实现更精确的视频生成控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Filmmaking demands precise motion control and reference image compositing -- capabilities that existing methods treat separately. Point-track-conditioned image-to-video models restrict content insertion to the first frame, while reference-to-video models lack fine-grained spatial-temporal control over how reference content integrates across frames.
  We present Go-with-the-Track, which unifies both capabilities by jointly conditioning on multiple reference images and reference-anchored point-tracks -- extending conventional point-tracks to explicitly establish correspondences between generated frames and reference images, thus enabling precise compositing and motion control throughout the video.
  To achieve this, we introduce spatially-aware point-track embeddings that encode the full sequence of point-track coordinates using a coordinate-wise MLP followed by temporal pooling. This representation captures the spatial characteristics of each point-track (serving as a unique identifier), while the embedding similarity correlates directly with spatial proximity, enhancing the model's ability to distinguish and associate point-tracks. We inject these point-track embeddings into a video diffusion transformer via a lightweight adapter, resolving the pixel-to-patch resolution mismatch while avoiding the substantial motion detail loss inherent in naive point-track subsampling.
  We use a hybrid training strategy to train jointly on dynamic, static, and synthetic scene video datasets to boost motion controllability. Experiments demonstrate that Go-with-the-Track achieves superior motion and reference control in a single model and enables new capabilities: multi-reference conditioned video generation with point-track driven compositing, as well as camera control for both static and dynamic scenes. Project Page: https://eyeline-labs.github.io/Go-with-the-Track/

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土地覆盖分割数据集在地理覆盖范围、规模或公共可获取性上存在局限，而RGB影像因成本与部署约束在实际应用中仍广泛使用，亟需一个大规模、公开的RGB遥感分割数据集来支持模型开发与跨区域泛化研究。

Conclusion: BELDE作为大陆尺度的RGB分割基准数据集，能够支持开发更鲁棒、可迁移的地球观测模型；数据集和基准资源将公开发布。

Abstract: Earth observation imagery plays a critical role in environmental monitoring, urban planning, disaster assessment, and climate analysis. While multi-spectral sensors are increasingly available, true-color (RGB) imagery remains widely used due to the power, cost, and deployment constraints of many satellite and aerial platforms. However, existing land-cover segmentation datasets are often limited in geographic coverage, scale, or public accessibility.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce BELDE (Building a Large-scale Earth-observation Land-cover Dataset for Europe), a publicly available dataset tailored for RGB-based remote sensing semantic segmentation. Constructed from Sentinel-2 true-color images and ESA WorldCover data annotations, BELDE contains 1,088,385 curated image-segmentation map pairs spanning Europe with 7 land-cover classes at 10 m spatial resolution, making it one of the largest publicly available RGB land-cover segmentation datasets for Earth observation. To facilitate cross-region generalization studies, we additionally introduce BELDE-K (16,607 pairs) covering the Republic of Korea and BELDE-CA-NV (88,155 pairs) covering California and Nevada in the United States.
  We establish baseline results using multiple semantic segmentation architectures and evaluate both in-domain and cross-domain performance. Models trained on BELDE achieve an F1 score of 83.0% on the European test set, while performance decreases to 66.4% on BELDE-CA-NV and 58.3% on BELDE-K, highlighting the challenges posed by out-of-distribution geographic domain shift. By providing a continental-scale RGB segmentation and evaluation benchmark, BELDE supports the development of robust and transferable Earth observation models. The dataset and benchmark resources will be publicly released.

</details>

*Abhijit Das,Nichula Wasalathilaka,Yifan Lu,Adinath Dukre,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Shadab Khan,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PROTON，一种基于原型在线测试时OOD检测方法，通过维护高置信度测试样本的原型库并自适应融合原型距离与MCM评分，在不修改模型、无需训练数据的情况下，显著提升了医学视觉语言模型在协变量偏移、语义偏移和远域OOD三种场景下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型（VLM）在零样本临床图像分类中，无论静态评分方法如何，都无法在所有分布偏移类型上可靠检测OOD输入。特别是协变量偏移下，传统MCM评分几乎完全失效（AUROC仅42.4%），而这种偏移在嵌入空间与softmax空间中存在结构不匹配——协变量偏移样本在softmax空间中与分布内样本不可区分，但在嵌入空间中占据不同区域。

Method: PROTON是一个轻量级后处理模块，核心包括：1) 在线原型库构建：从高置信度测试预测中维护原型；2) 自适应融合评分：通过流级方差统计，将原型距离与MCM评分动态融合，无需模型修改、训练数据或提示工程。

Result: 在眼科基准FLAIR + FIVES上，PROTON相比MCM在协变量偏移上提升+23.9 AUROC，语义偏移提升+8.8，远域OOD提升+8.1，是唯一无需分层提示或标注数据即可同时提升三种偏移检测性能的零样本方法。

Conclusion: PROTON有效利用了VLM嵌入空间中未被开发的结构信号，以轻量级后处理方式实现了对多种分布偏移类型的一致改进，为医学VLM的安全部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot clinical image classification, yet reliably detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs at deployment remains an open problem. No static scoring method works across all shift types: Maximum Concept Matching (MCM) on FLAIR achieves 76.4% AUROC for far-OOD but only 42.4% for covariate shifts such as ultra-wide-field fundus images, effectively random. We trace this to a structural mismatch: covariate-shifted inputs are indistinguishable from in-distribution samples in softmax space, yet occupy distinct regions in the VLM embedding space. To exploit this untapped signal, we propose PROTON (PROtotype-based Test-time ONline OOD detection), a lightweight post-hoc module that maintains an online prototype bank from high-confidence test predictions and adaptively fuses prototype distance with MCM scoring via stream-level variance statistics, requiring no model modification, training data, or prompt engineering. On the ophthalmology benchmark FLAIR + FIVES, PROTON improves MCM by +23.9 AUROC on covariate shift, +8.8 on semantic shift, and +8.1 on far-OOD, making it the only zero-shot method to improve all three without hierarchical prompts or labeled data. Code is available at https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/PROTON, and the project page is available at https://genmi-lab.github.io/PROTON.

</details>


### [47] [GIM-ENDO: A Multimodal Endoscopic Image and Video Dataset for Gastric Intestinal Metaplasia Morphology and Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20919)
*Mojgan Forootan,Mahziar Setayeshfar,Ali Darvishi,Mohammad Tashakoripour,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GIM-ENDO数据集，一个包含24名患者的胃黏膜肠上皮化生（GIM）内镜图像和视频数据集，提供详细的内镜征象标注、组织学亚型（完全型/不完全型）、OLGA/OLGIM分期等信息，旨在促进AI在GIM内镜检测中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃黏膜肠上皮化生是胃癌前病变，AI有望实现其内镜实时检测，但缺乏公开的、经病理验证的、包含详细内镜标注和组织学亚型的数据集，制约了可靠AI模型的开发。

Method: 使用Olympus EVIS X1系统采集24名患者（22例GIM阳性，2例正常对照）的白光内镜和图像增强内镜（含NBI和放大NBI）图像及视频，标注了6种主要IEE内镜征象（LBC、MTB、WOS、TV模式、萎缩、地图样红斑）及2种额外发现，标注GIM亚型（完全型/不完全型），并提供OLGA和OLGIM分期。

Result: 构建了GIM-ENDO数据集，包含人口学数据、内镜所见、病理结果、H. pylori状态及多媒体数据，已公开在Zenodo平台，并在DataBioX网站持续更新。

Conclusion: GIM-ENDO数据集填补了公开GIM内镜数据集的空白，为开发和验证AI驱动的GIM检测与分型模型提供了标准化资源，有助于推进胃癌前病变的早期诊断。

Abstract: Gastric intestinal metaplasia (GIM) is a precursor lesion to gastric dysplasia and adenocarcinoma whose early detection is crucial for intervening in the carcinogenesis cascade. Artificial intelligence (AI) holds considerable promise for real-time endoscopic detection and characterization of GIM. However, development of reliable AI models has been constrained by the absence of publicly available, histopathologically validated datasets that combine detailed endoscopic annotations, histological subtype (complete and incomplete), standardized grading systems, and normal mucosal patterns. GIM-ENDO was designed to fill this gap. The dataset comprises demographic data, endoscopic findings, histopathological results, and H. pylori status acquired using the Olympus EVIS X1 system with white-light endoscopy (WLE) and image-enhanced endoscopy (IEE), including narrow-band imaging (NBI) and magnifying NBI (M-NBI), along with images and video clips from 24 patients (22 GIM-positive, 2 normal controls). Annotations cover six primary IEE endoscopic signs -- light blue crest (LBC), marginal turbid band (MTB), white opaque substance (WOS), TV pattern (Fusion), atrophy, and map-like erythema (MLE) -- plus two additional endoscopic findings (AHP and GA) recorded where present. GIM subtypes (complete and incomplete) are annotated for all GIM-positive cases; OLGA and OLGIM staging are provided where complete histological sampling was available. The dataset is publicly accessible at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20707267. For the latest updates and further information regarding this dataset, readers are referred to the DataBioX website: https://databiox.com
  A short version of this work has been submitted to MICCAI 2026 Open Data Track.

</details>


### [48] [ELDiff: When Evidential Learning Meets Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20924)
*Qingtao Pan,Kai Ye,Zhihao Dou,Bing Ji,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ELDiff提出了一种基于证据学习的T2I扩散模型，通过不确定性度量和冲突检测增强多对象文本到图像生成中的语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在去噪过程中对文本与图像之间的语义一致性约束不足，而令牌监督模型虽能学习对象级一致性，但存在分割图偏差和语义重叠冲突问题，尤其是在涉及多个对象时。

Method: 提出ELDiff模型：1）像素证据损失（pixel evidence loss），通过证据正则化抑制不可靠标签的过度自信；2）令牌冲突损失（token conflict loss），通过优化测得的冲突因子来削弱语义间的矛盾。

Result: 在SD v1.4、SD v2.1、SDXL、SD v3.5和Qwen-Image上，ELDiff优于现有基于训练和无训练推理的T2I扩散模型，且无需额外的推理时操作。

Conclusion: ELDiff可无缝扩展到现有T2I扩散模型的训练流程中，有效提升多对象场景下的对象级语义一致性。

Abstract: In multi-object text-to-image (T2I) diffusion, ensuring semantic consistency between textual prompts and generated visual content is crucial for image synthesis. However, such consistency constraint is often underemphasized in the denoising process of diffusion models. Although token supervised diffusion models can mitigate this issue by learning object-wise consistency between the image content and object segmentation maps, it tends to suffer from the problems of segmentation map bias and semantic overlap conflict, especially when involving multiple objects. In this paper, we propose ELDiff, a new evidential learning-supervised T2I diffusion model, which leverages the advantages of uncertainty metric and conflict detection to enhance the fault tolerance of unreliable segmentation maps and suppress semantic conflicts, strengthening object-wise consistency learning. Specifically, a pixel evidence loss is proposed to restrain overconfidence in unreliable labels through evidential regularization, and a token conflict loss is designed to weaken the contradiction between semantics through optimizing a measured conflict factor. Extensive experiments show that our ELDiff outperforms existing training based and train-free based T2I diffusion models on SD v1.4, SD v2.1, SDXL, SD v3.5, and Qwen-Image, without requiring additional inference-time manipulations. Notably, ELDiff can be seamlessly extended to the existing training pipeline of T2I diffusion models. Code can be found at https://github.com/QingtaoPan/ELDiff.

</details>


### [49] [CogniRoute: Learning to Route Social Evidence in Omni-Modal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20970)
*Yifan Shen,Pei Tian,Xinzhuo Li,Bowen Fang,Shujun Xia,Bingxuan Li,Ana Jojic,Wenming Ye,Xu Cao,James Matthew Rehg,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CogniRoute，一个基于认知模式引导的混合专家框架，用于社交视频问答中的全模态推理。通过训练时引入的认知模式（分解跨模态关系、推理需求和时间范围），结合路由感知的强化学习，在OmniSocialBench基准上达到59.38%准确率，超过最强闭源模型15.33个百分点、开源全模态模型26.77个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态模型虽然能同时处理视频、音频和文本，但统一的多模态接入并不保证模型使用正确的证据进行推理。在社交视频问答中，答案可能依赖于手势、语气、时间线索或言语与视觉表达之间的不一致，现有模型缺乏对这种复杂社交情境的有效推理能力。

Method: 提出CogniRoute框架：(1) 训练仅使用的认知模式(cognitive schema)，分解每个样本的跨模态关系、推理需求和时间范围；(2) 在监督微调中将全局路由签名与认知模式对齐；(3) 引入路由感知的强化学习，联合优化token生成和专家分配，奖励包括答案正确性、模态一致推理和认知时间接地。同时构建OmniSocialBench数据集（118K结构化训练样本、推理轨迹、模式标签、时间证据跨度及人工验证评估集）。

Result: CogniRoute在OmniSocialBench上达到59.38%平均准确率，比最强闭源基线高15.33个百分点，比最强开源全模态基线高26.77个百分点。在需要音视频协调、冲突解决和时间接地社交推理的问题上提升最大。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模认知模式并结合路由感知的强化学习，可以显著提升全模态模型在社交视频问答中的推理能力，尤其是在需要多模态证据协调和时序理解的复杂场景中。

Abstract: Omni-modal models can ingest video, audio, and text, but unified access to multiple modalities does not guarantee that a model uses the right evidence. This gap is especially pronounced in social video question answering, where the answer may hinge on a gesture, vocal tone, temporal cue, or mismatch between what is said and what is visually expressed. We introduce CogniRoute, a schema-guided Mixture-of-Experts framework for social omni reasoning. CogniRoute uses a training-only cognitive schema that factorizes each example by cross-modal relation, reasoning demand, and temporal scope, and aligns global routing signatures with this structure during supervised fine-tuning. We further introduce route-aware reinforcement learning, which jointly optimizes token generation and expert allocation using rewards for answer correctness, modality-consistent reasoning, and cognitive temporal grounding. To support training and evaluation, we construct OmniSocialBench, a diagnostic social video QA resource with 118K structured training examples, grounded reasoning traces, schema labels, temporal evidence spans, and a manually verified evaluation split. CogniRoute achieves 59.38\% average accuracy on OmniSocialBench, improving over the strongest proprietary baseline by 15.33 percentage points and the strongest open-source omni baseline by 26.77 points, with the largest gains on questions requiring audio-visual coordination, conflict resolution, and temporally grounded social inference.

</details>


### [50] [UNITY: Attention Flow Networks for Adaptive Conditioning in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20971)
*Aryan Das,Koushik Biswas,Moloud Abdar,Vinay Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UNITY提出了一种通用到特化的适配器方法，通过两阶段训练范式（通用阶段+特化阶段）和Morphable Attention Flow网络，实现高效、可扩展的多模态条件图像生成，在保持高图像质量的同时显著降低推理延迟和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件扩散模型通常为每种条件模态训练独立的适配器，缺乏跨模态共享语义的学习，导致参数冗余和扩展性差。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1) 通用阶段（占一半训练步数），学习所有条件模态的跨模态共享表示；2) 特化阶段，在剩余训练预算下细化模态特定特征。核心模块包括Morphable Attention Flow (MAF) Network和Morph Wrapper，通过可学习流场和注意力融合实现通道感知与空间自适应的特征对齐。

Result: 在多个数据集上达到最先进的图像保真度，同时保持优越的内存效率，且常复杂度的设计支持单条件和复合条件设置下的灵活操作。

Conclusion: UNITY通过共享语义学习与特化微调的结合，实现了跨模态条件扩散模型的高效、可扩展生成，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce UNITY, a Universal-to-Specialized adapter for efficient and scalable composite conditioning in diffusion based image generation. Unlike prior methods that train separate adapters for each conditioning modality, UNITY jointly learns shared semantics across multiple conditioning types and subsequently specializes without modifying the underlying architecture. The proposed two stage training paradigm consists of a Universal Stage that captures cross modal representations across all conditioning modalities using half of the total training steps, followed by a Specialization Stage that refines modality specific features using the remaining training budget. At the core of UNITY are the Morphable Attention Flow (MAF) Network and Morph Wrapper modules, which enable channel aware and spatially adaptive feature alignment through learnable flow fields and attention based fusion. This constant complexity formulation supports flexible operation under both single and composite conditioning settings while significantly reducing inference latency and memory consumption. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that UNITY achieves state of the art image fidelity while maintaining superior memory efficiency. Code: https://github.com/arya-domain/UNITY

</details>


### [51] [Robusto-2: Benchmarking Humans & VLMs for Autonomous Driving in Lima & New York City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20980)
*Adrian Cespedes,Marcelo Chincha,Dunant Cusipuma,Victor Flores-Benites,David Ortega,Arturo Deza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比利马和纽约的人类驾驶员与VLM在多模态VQA任务上的表现，发现人类（无论来自哪个城市）与VLM的回答存在差异，但地理因素对回答的调节作用并不显著，这可能是由于两地均属于高度OOD的驾驶环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶系统越来越多地使用VLM作为认知骨干，研究这些模型在OOD边缘场景（尤其是新地理区域）中的泛化能力是一个关键但尚未充分探索的问题。

Method: 采用全因子实验设计，招募利马本地驾驶员、纽约市驾驶员以及VLM，观看来自利马和纽约的行车记录仪视频，并在VQA范式下回答涵盖事实性、评级、反事实和推理四类问题。

Result: 人类与VLM的回答存在显著差异，且差异受问题类型调节；来自不同地理区域的人类驾驶员回答表现出相似性；未发现地理因素对人类或VLM的回答产生强调节作用。

Conclusion: 在高度OOD的驾驶场景下，当前VLM与人类驾驶员的认知判断存在分歧，且这种分歧并非简单地由地理位置差异驱动，暗示VLM在跨地理区域的边缘场景泛化方面仍需改进。

Abstract: As Self-Driving Cars continue to expand internationally and use multi-modal systems such as VLMs as a cognitive backbone for their Action models; how well will these systems generalize in new settings, in particular out-of-distribution (OOD) edge-case scenarios in new geographies? In this paper, we study this open question by providing a full factorial analysis with human drivers of Lima, human drivers from New York City, and VLMs and showing them dashcam footage collected from Lima and New York City -- prompting them with a variety of questions under a Visual Question Answering (VQA) paradigm. In particular, we pick these two cities as they are highly challenging driving locations where no Self-Driving Car company currently operates in, and ask questions that span 4 categories: Factual, Ratings, Counterfactual and Reasoning. We find that Humans and VLMs diverge in their responses -- though this is modulated by the type of questions asked, and that Humans answer similarly independent of where they are from (Lima/NYC). To our surprise, we did not find a strong difference in terms of answers (Humans or VLMs) that was modulated by geography, likely due to their high out-of-distribution nature. Our dataset is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Artificio/robusto-2

</details>


### [52] [CheXpercept: A Benchmark for Evaluating Expert-Level Lesion Perception in Chest X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21020)
*Geon Choi,Hangyul Yoon,Nalee Kim,Jeong Yun Jang,Hyunju Shin,Hyunki Park,Sang Hoon Seo,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CheXpercept基准，通过粗粒度检测、细粒度轮廓评估与修订、语义级属性提取三个层次评估视觉-语言模型在胸部X光片中的病灶感知能力，发现现有模型仅在粗粒度任务上表现尚可，医学VLM相比通用VLM并无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对胸部X光片视觉-语言模型的评估局限于疾病分类任务，缺乏对视觉定位和病灶感知能力的验证，无法确保模型的临床可靠性。

Method: 构建CheXpercept基准，包含粗粒度检测、细粒度轮廓评估与修订、语义级属性提取三个层次；采用半自动生成管线配合六位医学专家审核构建包含10,400个QA项、2,100张CXR的数据集；对14个通用和医学VLM进行基准测试。

Result: 模型仅在粗粒度任务上表现尚可，在深层视觉任务上精度急剧下降；医学VLM相比通用VLM几乎无感知优势。

Conclusion: 当前领域自适应存在系统性缺陷，医学VLM的临床级病灶感知能力仍需大幅提升。

Abstract: The evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) for chest X-ray (CXR) analysis has largely been limited to disease-presence classification without visual grounding. Such evaluations fail to verify the expert-level lesion perception necessary to ensure the clinical reliability of VLMs. To address these limitations, we introduce CheXpercept, a sequential, multi-level perception benchmark that mirrors a radiologist's cognitive workflow across coarse-level detection, fine-level contour evaluation and revision, and semantic-level attribute extraction. To ensure high clinical fidelity at scale, we construct the dataset using a semi-automated generation pipeline paired with a review by six medical experts. CheXpercept contains 10,400 QA items derived from 2,100 CXRs, covering seven clinically critical pulmonary and cardiac lesions. To demonstrate the current landscape of VLM perception, we benchmark 14 general and medical VLMs on CheXpercept. The models achieve adequate performance only at the coarse level, with accuracy degrading precipitously on deeper visual tasks. Notably, medical VLMs show almost no perceptual advantage over their general-domain counterparts, highlighting a systemic flaw in current domain adaptation. The code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [53] [Sparse Point-Guided Fusion of Supervised and Self-Supervised Learning Model for Seaweed Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21026)
