We introduce DynamicMem, a synthetic benchmark that constructs 15 months of activity per user, providing long-term multi-app data that real users' privacy keeps out of reach. It provides user-consistent trajectories averaging 2.2M tokens and 1,772 grounded events per user across 16 applications such as e-commerce, fitness, and social platforms. The profile evolves over this period and is never given explicitly: each attribute, habit, or preference must be inferred from small signals scattered across apps. We evaluate at five quarterly checkpoints to track how systems scale as history grows. Benchmarking five representative systems exposes problems a single accuracy score hides: (i) profile reconstruction degrades with history length while service-task accuracy stays flat, despite both drawing on the same memory; (ii) no system both keeps facts that stay true and replaces facts that change, with errors clustering on preferences and on naming the exact referent; and (iii) over 93% of failures trace to what the memory retrieves, not to the model writing the answer, so the largest room for improvement lies in memory itself. Code: https://wenyaxie023.github.io/DynamicMem/

</details>


### [271] [Explanation-Guided Medical Named Entity Recognition with Stability and Boundary Awareness for Atopic Dermatitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22886)
*Xueguang Li,Di Lin,Xue Jiang,Yanxi Li,Yugang Chi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解释引导学习的稳定性和边界感知框架，用于提升中文特应性皮炎临床文本中医学命名实体识别的可靠性与鲁棒性，通过融合局部与全局解释信号并引入稳定性、边界感知和一致性约束，实验表明该方法在多个NER模型上均取得一致性性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中文特应性皮炎临床文本中医学命名实体识别的可靠性和鲁棒性，解决现有NER模型在医学领域可解释性不足的问题。

Method: 提出稳定性和边界感知的解释引导NER框架：采用基于扰动的分析评估解释稳定性与实体边界敏感性；设计自适应融合策略动态结合局部与全局解释生成更可靠的词级解释；通过稳定性约束、边界感知约束和一致性约束将解释信号融入模型训练。

Result: 在中文AD NER数据集上，该框架提升了解释鲁棒性，在多个NER模型上取得一致性能提升；自适应融合策略比单一解释方法提供更稳定的解释和更强的边界感知能力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效将可靠解释信号集成到医学NER训练中，同时提升识别性能和解释可靠性，为可解释医学NER提供了实用且可泛化的解决方案，为下游临床决策和医学知识应用提供可靠支持。

Abstract: Objective: This study aims to improve the reliability and robustness of medical named entity recognition (NER) in Chinese atopic dermatitis (AD) clinical texts through explanation-guided learning. Methods: We propose a stability and boundary-aware explanation-guided NER framework. Perturbation-based analysis is used to evaluate explanation stability and entity boundary sensitivity. An adaptive fusion strategy dynamically combines local and global explanation to generate more reliable token-level explanations. The fused explanation signals are further incorporated into model training through stability, boundary-aware, and consistency constraints. Results: Experiments on Chinese AD NER datasets show that the proposed framework improves explanation robustness and achieves consistent performance gains across multiple NER models. The adaptive fusion strategy also provides more stable explanations and stronger boundary perception than individual explanation methods. Conclusion: The proposed method effectively integrates reliable explanation signals into medical NER training, improving both recognition performance and explanation reliability. The framework provides a practical and generalizable solution for explainable medical NER and offers reliable support for downstream clinical decision-making and medical knowledge applications.

</details>


### [272] [Understanding Knowledge Distillation in Post-Training: When It Helps and When It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22942)
*Xin Liu,Simin Ma,Shujian Liu,Song Wang,Sathish Reddy Indurthi,Haoyun Deng,Lu Wang,Kaiqiang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了知识蒸馏在大规模后训练中的应用，发现KD在低数据场景下优于SFT，但优势随数据增加而减弱；从更强的指令微调教师模型蒸馏即使在数据充足时也能带来显著收益；提出两阶段KD策略，在低资源场景下持续提升学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs计算成本高，难以部署在资源受限环境。KD虽被提出作为解决方案，但先前研究主要关注任务特定或小规模场景，对后训练阶段构建通用指令遵循模型的KD研究有限。

Method: 使用大规模Tulu 3数据集系统研究KD在后训练中的效果；比较KD与SFT在不同数据规模下的表现；分析教师模型强度对KD效果的影响；针对低资源场景提出两阶段KD策略（先用合成教师标注数据，再用人工标注数据精炼）。

Result: KD在低数据场景下优于SFT，但优势随数据增加减弱；从更强指令微调教师蒸馏能在数据充足时恢复显著收益；两阶段KD策略在资源稀缺场景下持续提升学生性能。

Conclusion: KD在数据稀缺场景下是构建轻量级模型的有效方法；当教师提供学生无法从训练数据中轻易获取的知识时，KD始终有效；提出的两阶段策略为数据稀缺环境下构建紧凑模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their high computational cost limits deployment in resource-constrained environments. Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a teacher model of a larger size to a smaller student model. While prior work has mainly examined task-specific or small-scale settings, the post-training stage for building general instruction-following models has received limited attention. In this paper, we conduct a systematic study of KD in post-training using the large-scale Tulu 3 dataset. We find that KD outperforms supervised fine-tuning (SFT) in low-data regimes, but its advantage diminishes as more training data is added. Distilling from a stronger instruction-tuned teacher restores substantial gains even with abundant data, indicating that KD remains effective when the teacher provides knowledge that the student cannot easily acquire from the training data alone. We further study domain-specific, low-resource scenarios and propose a two-stage KD strategy that leverages synthetic teacher-labeled data followed by refinement on human annotations. This method consistently improves student performance, providing practical guidance for building compact models in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [273] [StatABench: Dataset and Framework for Evaluating Statistical Analysis Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22977)
*Youxin Zhu,Yixuan Ding,Peng Lai,Longyue Wang,Bingyi Jing,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了StatABench基准，用于系统评估大语言模型在统计分析方面的能力，包含404道封闭式题目和30道开放式建模任务。实验表明最佳模型GPT-5.1在封闭式任务上仅达68.6%，最佳开源模型为60.6%，开放式任务最高平均分61.86，揭示了当前LLM在统计分析方面与可靠水平之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在统计领域的评估基准在范围和格式上存在局限性，缺乏系统性评估LLM统计分析能力的全面基准。

Method: 设计StatABench，包含Stat-Closed（404道题，涵盖18个统计主题，多种格式）和Stat-Open（30个源自专业竞赛的复杂开放式建模任务）。采用LangChain MCP框架和数据科学智能体评估各类LLM，并通过LLM-as-Judge协议评估开放式解答。

Result: GPT-5.1在Stat-Closed上仅68.6%，最佳开源模型60.6%；Stat-Open上最佳智能体框架平均分61.86。

Conclusion: 当前LLM与可靠统计分析之间存在显著差距，在基于工具的推理、方法论决策和端到端统计建模方面仍面临持续挑战。

Abstract: Statistical analysis is a broad, complex field requiring both domain knowledge and tool proficiency. While prior work has evaluated large language models (LLMs) in this domain, existing benchmarks remain limited in scope and format. To bridge this gap, we introduce StatABench (Statistical AnalysisBenchmark), a benchmark designed to systematically assess LLMs' statistical analysis capabilities. StatABench comprises two complementary components: Stat-Closed, containing 404 questions across 18 statistical topics in multiple formats (multiple-choice, fill-in-the-blank, decision-making, and practical application), and Stat-Open, featuring 30 complex open-ended modeling tasks adapted from professional competitions. We evaluate diverse LLMs using the LangChain MCP framework and multiple data science agents, and assess Stat-Open solutions via a validated LLM-as-Judge protocol. Experiments show that even GPT-5.1 achieves only 68.6% on Stat-Closed, while the best open-source model reaches 60.6%. On Stat-Open, the top agent framework scores 61.86 on average. These results reveal the gap between current LLMs and reliable statistical analysis, highlighting persistent challenges in tool-grounded reasoning, methodological decision-making, and end-to-end statistical modeling.

</details>


### [274] [Predicate Importance Estimation and Decoupled Rationale-Score Distillation for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22992)
*Keunha Kim,Yoonjin Jang,Hyeon-gu Lee,Sihyung Kim,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对工业级KG-RAG系统中多源知识图谱实体对齐问题，提出谓词重要性估计（PIE）和去耦理由-分数蒸馏（DRSD）两个互补模块。PIE通过去除主语信息的谓词感知嵌入学习实体表示，DRSD利用教师LLM生成伪答案训练小语言模型实现置信度与理由的去耦学习，显著提升对齐性能并支持人机协同验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级KG-RAG系统需要整合来自异构数据库的公共和领域特定知识图谱，而实体对齐（EA）是这一整合的核心挑战。现有方法在谓词名称变化和不完整的局部邻域条件下，仅靠词汇匹配无法有效解决EA问题。

Method: 构建成对EA数据集，提出两个互补模块：1）PIE：去除每个1跳三元组的主语信息，编码无主语三元组，并通过可学习的谓词重要性权重聚合，构建谓词感知的实体嵌入；2）DRSD：通过教师LLM使用不同提示生成伪答案，训练一个蒸馏的小语言模型（SLM），将二元EA标签转换为文本监督，并解耦置信度评分估计与标签一致的理由生成。

Result: 实验表明PIE和DRSD均能提升EA分类性能。DRSD因解耦置信度估计与决策，两者间的差异可标记不确定预测供人工审核，实现了自动接受与人在回路验证之间的实用差异机制。

Conclusion: PIE和DRSD两个模块有效解决了知识图谱整合中的实体对齐问题，特别是DRSD的置信度-理由解耦设计不仅提升了模型性能，还为实际部署中自动决策与人工复核的协同提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are increasingly used as structured context for Large Language Models (LLMs), but industrial KG-RAG systems often need to integrate public and domain-specific KGs constructed from heterogeneous databases. This integration relies on Entity Alignment (EA), where lexical matching alone is insufficient under predicate-name variation and incomplete local neighborhoods. We address EA for KG integration by constructing a pairwise EA dataset and proposing two complementary modules: Predicate Importance Estimation (PIE) and Decoupled Rationale-Score Distillation (DRSD). PIE is a compact embedding-based approach that removes the subject information from each 1-hop triple, encodes the resulting subjectless triples, and aggregates them with learnable predicate-importance weights to build predicate-aware entity embeddings. DRSD trains a distilled small language model (SLM) with pseudo-answers produced by a teacher LLM through distinct prompts. By converting binary EA labels into text-based supervision and decoupling confidence-score estimation from label-consistent rationales, DRSD enables the SLM to learn task-specific reasoning while retaining a less label-biased confidence signal. Experiments show that PIE and DRSD improve EA classification. Moreover, because DRSD decouples confidence-score estimation from the decision, a discrepancy between the two flags an uncertain prediction for human review, thereby enabling a practical discrepancy between automatic acceptance and human-in-the-loop verification.

</details>


### [275] [Machine Translation and Post-Editing: Comparative Evaluation of Different MT Systems and Post-Editor Groups in Specialised Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23002)
*Joachim Minder,Alexandra Mestivier,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种机器翻译系统（DeepL、eTranslation、Systran）在英译法专业文本翻译中的质量，并比较了语言学家/译者和NLP专家两组后编辑者的表现，发现系统间和编辑者组间在术语准确性和流畅性方面存在显著差异，强调了领域知识在专业翻译中的重要性以及MT系统在专门用途语言中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估机器翻译及后编辑在专业翻译（英译法）中的实际质量，比较不同MT系统的表现以及不同背景后编辑者的效果差异，以揭示领域知识对专业翻译的重要性。

Method: 使用三种MT系统（DeepL、eTranslation、Systran）生成译文，分别由语言学家/译者和NLP专家两组后编辑者进行编辑，采用适用于MT和PE评估的错误类型标注框架进行翻译质量评估。

Result: 三种MT系统之间表现出显著差异；两组后编辑者之间也存在显著差异，尤其在术语准确性和流畅性维度上表现突出。

Conclusion: 专业翻译中领域知识至关重要；MT系统在专门用途语言（LSP）场景下表现不稳定且存在局限性。

Abstract: This article aims to evaluate the quality of machine translation (MT) and post-editing (PE) in the context of specialised translation from English into French. Three MT systems (DeepL, eTranslation and Systran) were compared, and two groups of post-editors -linguists/translators and NLP experts -were asked to perform post-editing. Translation assessment is based on error annotation using an error typology adapted to MT and PE evaluation. The results reveal significant differences between the three MT systems and the two groups of post-editors, particularly in terms of terminological accuracy and fluency. This study highlights the importance of domain knowledge in specialised translation, as well as the limitations and variable performance of MT systems in language for specific purposes (LSP).

</details>


### [276] [Have You Ever Seen Them? Entity-level Membership Inference through Interrogating Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23030)
*Yiran Zhu,Ziqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出实体级别的成员推理攻击，判断LLM是否在训练中接触过特定实体的相关信息。在仅有输出文本可观测的黑盒设定下，基于有限线索设计五种询问策略，通过语义特征实现高效推理，在人物实体上AUC达0.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理研究主要关注样本级别的训练数据记忆，但LLM可能不逐字记忆特定样本，却能通过分散提及累积并泄露关于真实世界实体的知识。这种类似人类记忆的行为未被充分研究，因此需要探索实体级别的成员推理方法。

Method: 形式化定义了实体级成员推理任务，在标签-only黑盒设定下建立必要与充分条件。基于此设计五种询问策略，利用有限实体线索构造提示，引导模型生成与实体相关的文本，再从生成文本中提取语义特征进行成员关系推断。

Result: 在人物实体数据集上，AUC可达0.97，相比最优基线方法在平衡准确率上提升6.0%-17.5%。

Conclusion: 实体级成员推理是可行且有效的，揭示了LLM在实体层面存在类似人类记忆的信息暴露风险，为隐私评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) raise growing concerns about privacy leakage and copyright compliance. Membership inference is a key tool for assessing such risks, but existing studies mainly focus on whether specific samples or sample-based data units are used for training. We argue that LLMs exhibit a human-memory-like behavior: an LLM may not memorize a specific sample verbatim, yet it can accumulate and reveal knowledge about a real-world entity from scattered mentions. This analogy motivates us to examine whether an LLM can be interrogated like a human interviewee to reveal its exposure to entity-related information. Motivated by this question, we propose entity-level membership inference, which determines whether information related to a target entity is used in LLM training. We study this task in the practical label-only black-box setting, where only generated texts are observable. We formalize the task under clue, input, and model constraints, establish the necessary and sufficient conditions for its feasibility, and instantiate five interrogation strategies based on this formalization. The strategies use limited entity clues to construct prompts, elicit entity-related responses, and infer membership from semantic features among the generated texts. We construct entity-level datasets and adapt state-of-the-art sample-level label-only methods to the entity-level setting as baselines. Experiments on person entities show that our methods achieve AUC up to 0.97 and bring gains of 6.0%--17.5% in Balanced Accuracy over the best adapted baseline.

</details>


### [277] [Training Open Models for Agentic Phone Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23049)
*Zhengyang Tang,Xin Lai,Pengyuan Lyu,Xinyuan Wang,Tianyi Bai,Chenxin Li,Yiduo Guo,Huawen Shen,Yuxuan Liu,Junyi Li,Zhengyao Fang,Yang Ding,Yi Zhang,Weinong Wang,Xingran Zhou,Liang Wu,Fei Tang,Sunqi Fan,Shangpin Peng,Zheng Ruan,Anran Zhang,Benyou Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Rui Yan,Chengquan Zhang,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhoneBuddy提出了一种结合真实应用环境和模拟应用环境（PhoneWorld）的训练方案，通过混合强化学习提升手机智能体任务成功率。在150项真实手机人工评估中，任务成功率从SFT后的36.67%提升至混合RL的45.33%；在AndroidWorld上从60.3%提升至83.2%。模拟环境并非替代真实环境RL，而是提供可扩展、可重置、可自动验证的互补训练来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机正成为通用智能体的重要执行平台，但训练可靠的开放模型面临困境：真实设备环境运行缓慢、有状态、有副作用、难以重置和验证，而可扩展的模拟环境只能近似真实行为，两者之间存在差距。

Method: 提出PhoneBuddy训练方案：1) 构建PhoneWorld模拟环境，从真实GUI使用结构重建可运行的模拟应用；2) 先在真实环境和模拟环境收集轨迹进行共享监督微调（SFT）；3) 对比纯真实环境RL与混合环境RL的效果。

Result: 150项真实手机人工评估：SFT后36.67% → 真实RL后40.67% → 混合RL后45.33%；AndroidWorld上：60.3% → 77.2% → 83.2%。增益主要集中在应用和小型应用任务，跨应用长流程任务仍是开放挑战。

Conclusion: 模拟环境训练并非真实环境RL的替代品，而是可扩展、可重置、可自动验证的互补训练来源。混合RL是最优策略。

Abstract: Phones are becoming an important execution surface for general-purpose agents, but training open models for reliable phone use remains difficult because the environment that matters at deployment, real devices running real apps, is slow, stateful, side-effectful, and hard to reset or verify, while scalable mock environments only approximate real behavior. We present PhoneBuddy, a training recipe and open-model line for agentic phone use that combines a real-app environment with a mock-app environment, PhoneWorld, which reconstructs runnable mock apps from real GUI usage structure. PhoneBuddy first builds a shared supervised fine-tuning stage from trajectories collected in both environments, then compares real-app RL against mixed RL across both environments. Across a 150-task human evaluation on real phones spanning apps, mini-apps, and cross-app workflows, task success rate improves from 36.67\% after supervised fine-tuning to 40.67\% after real-app RL and 45.33\% after mixed RL. On AndroidWorld, the same progression rises from 60.3\% to 77.2\% to 83.2\%. These results show that mock-app training is not a replacement for real-app RL, but a complementary source of scalable, resettable, and automatically checked interaction. The gains are strongest on app and mini-app tasks, while long-horizontal cross-app workflows remain an important open challenge.

</details>


### [278] [PIVOTSBench: Evaluating Fine-Grained Interpersonal Relationship Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23092)
*Shuxiang Zhang,Yiting Yin,Wenxuan Song,Yuhang Wu,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PIVOTS基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)理解双向人际关系维度的能力，该基准基于Social-IQ 2.0和YouTube数据构建，并包含辅助任务来评估模型识别关键视觉线索的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类天生具备理解细粒度人际关系的能力，这是日常社交互动的核心，但现有MLLMs在这方面尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了PIVOTS基准，包含双向人际关系维度预测的主任务和识别关键视觉线索的辅助任务，并设计了联合预测和成对预测两种设置。

Result: 评估了多种闭源和开源MLLMs，通过消融实验分析了视觉模态和显式社交角色信息的影响，并验证了联合预测和成对预测设置的优势。

Conclusion: PIVOTS填补了MLLMs在细粒度人际关系理解方面的评估空白，揭示了当前模型在这一多模态社交推理任务上的局限性。

Abstract: Humans possess an innate ability to understand fine-grained interpersonal relationships, which is central to everyday social interactions. Although such reasoning is inherently multimodal, it remains largely unexplored by existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To address this gap, we introduce PIVOTS, the first benchmark built from Social-IQ 2.0 and YouTube data to evaluate MLLMs' ability to predict bidirectional interpersonal relationship dimensions grounded in established psychology research. In addition, PIVOTS includes auxiliary tasks that assess models' ability to identify and leverage the critical visual cues underlying such predictions. We evaluate both proprietary and open-source MLLMs and conduct detailed ablation studies to analyze the effects of visual modalities and explicit social role information in conversational utterances. We further examine how joint and pairwise prediction settings benefit MLLMs in scoring bidirectional PIVOTS dimensions. Project page and resources: https://flynnzhangsx.github.io/PIVOTSBench/ .

</details>


### [279] [A Dual-Track Framework for Template-Constrained LaTeX Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23107)
*Chung Cheuk Hei,Liu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种双轨框架，将模板格式化与文档处理解耦：离线轨道提取模板约束为可复用的清单，在线轨道通过混合执行流水线将LLM限制在推理密集型任务，将确定性处理交由规则引擎。在7个LaTeX模板和56篇论文上的实验表明，该方法在结构保真度、布局约束满足和编译成功率方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在缺陷：基于规则的转换器无法正确处理资产插入和模板特定约束；纯端到端LLM生成则会导致语义漂移和幻觉，且难以调试。

Method: 提出Dual-Track Framework：离线轨道将模板约束提取为可复用清单；在线轨道采用混合执行流水线，将LLM限制用于推理密集型组件（语义元数据、参考文献、复杂视觉/表格布局），将确定性处理交由规则引擎。

Result: 在7个LaTeX模板和56篇已发表研究论文上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法，在结构保真度、布局约束满足和编译成功率方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 该双轨框架通过解耦模板格式化与文档处理，结合LLM的推理能力与规则引擎的确定性优势，有效解决了Markdown到LaTeX转换中的结构保真和模板合规问题。

Abstract: With the increasing demands for advanced document conversion, mapping structured Markdown drafts into template-compliant formats like LaTeX remains a challenge. Existing approaches largely depend on either deterministic rule-based converters or pure end-to-end Large Language Model (LLM) generation. The former fails to correctly handle asset insertions and template-specific constraints, while the latter tends to induce semantic drift, leading to hallucinations that are difficult to debug. To address these limitations, we introduce a robust Dual-Track Framework that systematically decouples template formatting from document processing: an offline track extracts template constraints into a reusable manifest, while an online track implements a hybrid execution pipeline. This pipeline confines LLM usage exclusively to reasoning-intensive components (e.g., semantic metadata, bibliographic references, and complex visual/tabular layouts) while delegating rule-based engines for deterministic processing. Empirical evaluation across 7 LaTeX templates and 56 published research papers demonstrates that our method preserves better structural fidelity, satisfies diverse layout constraints, and achieves a higher compilation success rate compared to the previous baselines.

</details>


### [280] [PRIDE: Privileged Information-enhanced Distillation for Empathetic Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23124)
*Jiaqiang Wu,Zhouan Zhu,Shangfei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PRIDE方法，利用训练时可用的特权信息（如心理标注或未来事件摘要）通过知识蒸馏将大语言模型的共情推理能力转移到小模型上，在资源受限环境中实现高效共情对话生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在共情对话中表现出色但计算开销巨大，难以部署在资源受限环境；现有知识蒸馏方法未能有效传递共情所需的细微理解，因为它们忽略了引导人类连接的隐含上下文线索。

Method: PRIDE包含三个核心组件：(1) 共情推理提示，引导教师模型逐步分解共情过程（理解感受+分析情境）；(2) 多源注意力机制，指导学生模型有效整合特权信息；(3) 双对齐损失函数，结合反向KL散度和最大均值差异，在logit和特征两个层面实现稳健知识迁移。

Result: 在多模态和纯文本数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到了有竞争力的性能，在某些情况下甚至匹配或超越更大的教师模型，在准确性和语义相关性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 利用训练时特权信息进行知识蒸馏可以有效弥合大模型与小模型之间的共情能力差距，使小模型在不依赖额外推理输入的情况下也能生成高质量的共情回应。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated significant capabilities in generating diverse and context-aware responses for empathetic dialogue. However, their computational demands severely limit their deployment in resource-constrained environments. While knowledge distillation offers a promising compression solution, it often fails to transfer the nuanced understanding essential for empathy, as it overlooks the implicit contextual cues that guide human connection. To bridge this gap, we propose a \textbf{pr}ivileged \textbf{i}nformation-enhanced knowledge \textbf{d}istillation method for \textbf{e}mpathetic dialogue generation (PRIDE). Our method leverages privileged information, such as expert psychological annotations or future event summaries, which is available exclusively during training but unavailable at inference time. This allows us to transfer the teacher model's empathetic reasoning to smaller models without relying on extra inputs during deployment. Specifically, PRIDE has three key components: (1) An empathy-reasoning prompt that guides the teacher to explicitly decompose the empathetic process into understanding feelings and analyzing situations step-by-step; (2) A multi-source attention mechanism that directs the student to effectively integrate privileged information; (3) A dual-alignment loss that combines reversed Kullback-Leibler divergence and maximum mean discrepancy to ensure robust knowledge transfer at both logit and feature levels. Experiments on multi-modal and text-only datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance, and in some cases matches or even surpasses larger teacher models in terms of accuracy and semantic relevance.

</details>


### [281] [Same question, different history: language, national identity, and credit in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23164)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault,Wei Zhang,Vitor D. de Moura,José O. Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在回答"谁发明了什么"这类争议性问题时，查询语言会系统性地影响模型呈现的答案：使用某种语言提问时，与该语言相关的非主流主张者更可能被提及，而英语主导人物则保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否以及如何在不同语言中呈现具有国家争议的历史发明归属问题，揭示语言作为"开关"如何激活不同版本的民族历史记忆。

Method: 分析11个主流大语言模型，覆盖21项有争议的发明/发现，在12种语言下评估，共收集75,896条回答；控制响应长度、模型差异、历史知名度和国家纪念水平等变量。

Result: 查询语言显著影响模型呈现的争议主张者：低地位主张者在其相关语言提问下更常出现，而英语主导人物跨语言保持稳定；这一模式在控制多个混淆变量后依然成立。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型充当了分布式文化记忆系统，语言条件决定了哪些历史版本变得可见，构成了一种计算形式的"平庸民族主义"（banal nationalism）。

Abstract: Who invented the radio, Russia's Alexander Popov or Italy's Guglielmo Marconi? Was the telephone the achievement of Bell in the United States or Meucci in Italy? Does printing belong to China's Bi Sheng or Germany's Gutenberg? The answer depends not only on historical record but also on language and perspective.
  We analyse eleven widely used large language models across 21 disputed inventions and discoveries, evaluated in twelve languages and 75,896 responses. While models generally acknowledge that credit is contested, query language systematically affects which claimant is surfaced. Lower-status claimants are more likely to appear when questions are asked in their associated language, whereas dominant Anglophone figures remain stable across languages.
  These patterns persist after controlling for response length, model differences, historical prominence, and levels of national commemoration. Language thus acts as a switch that activates different national versions of the same history, producing systematically different national memories from the same question.
  We interpret this as evidence that large language models function as distributed systems of cultural memory, where language conditions which histories become visible, contributing to a computational form of banal nationalism.

</details>


### [282] [When Does Intrinsic Self-Correction Help? A Task-Sensitive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23196)
*Elroy Stav,Dvir Berlowitz,Maayan Orner,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务敏感视角下的内在自我修正（SC）研究，发现SC并非普遍可靠，其效果取决于任务结构是否支持验证约束、复查推理或提供竞争策略的第二意见这三种修正机制，在适宜任务中可带来稳定性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明大语言模型的内在自我修正（不依赖外部反馈）可靠性存疑，模型难以判断自身初始回答是否正确。本文旨在超越"SC是否有效"的笼统问题，探索SC在不同任务结构下的工作机制和适用条件。

Method: 采用任务敏感视角，将SC分为三种机制：验证显式约束、复查复杂推理过程、在文字游戏中提供竞争策略的第二意见。在多个基准和模型上系统评估SC在不同任务结构下的表现。

Result: 当底层任务结构有利于上述修正模式时，SC能够带来一致的性能提升；反之则效果有限。

Conclusion: 内在自我修正应被理解为一种任务依赖的推理时策略，其有用性取决于修正阶段在特定任务中能发挥的作用，而非一种普遍可靠的初始输出改进方法。

Abstract: Intrinsic self-correction (SC) aims to improve large language model outputs by prompting a model to revisit its own initial answer without external feedback. Recent studies have questioned the reliability of this approach, showing that models often struggle to judge whether their initial responses are correct. In this work, we take a task-sensitive view of SC. Rather than asking whether it works in general, we examine settings where SC may operate through different mechanisms: verifying explicit constraints, revisiting a complex reasoning process, or providing a second opinion over competing strategies in word-game tasks. Across multiple benchmarks and models, we find that SC can yield consistent performance gains when the underlying task structure facilitates these modes of revision. These results suggest that SC is best understood as a task-dependent inference-time strategy whose usefulness depends on the role the revision stage can play in a given task, rather than as a uniformly reliable method for improving initial model outputs.

</details>


### [283] [MuPPET: A Benchmark for Contextual Privacy of LLM Assistants in Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23217)
*Elena Sofia Ruzzetti,Cornelius Emde,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh,Martin Gubri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MuPPET是一个用于评估多主体对话中上下文隐私泄露的基准测试。实验发现，模型在多主体场景中的隐私泄露远高于一对一场景，前沿模型和小型开源模型都存在漏洞，现有防御措施效果有限且会降低实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私基准仅考虑单一对话者场景，未能衡量多主体对话中的隐私风险，而LLM代理越来越多地部署在多主体环境中处理敏感个人数据。

Method: 提出MuPPET（多主体隐私暴露测试）基准，用于评估多主体对话中的上下文隐私泄露情况。

Result: 模型在多主体设置中的隐私泄露显著高于一对一评估的结果；前沿模型存在漏洞，小型开源模型（常用于本地部署敏感数据）更为严重；现有上下文隐私防御仅提供部分保护，降低实用性，且未解决底层的主体追踪问题。

Conclusion: 多主体对话中的隐私风险比一对一设置更严重，现有防御措施不足，需要新的解决方案来应对主体追踪这一核心问题。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-party environments, handling sensitive personal data on behalf of individual users, for instance in group chats. When such an agent discloses private information, it reaches every group member at once. This risk is structurally harder to control than in one-to-one settings, as every piece of private information must be appropriate for every recipient in the group. Yet all existing contextual privacy benchmarks consider only single-interlocutor settings, leaving multi-party privacy risks unmeasured. We introduce MuPPET (Multi-Party Privacy Exposure Testing), a benchmark for contextual privacy in multi-party conversations. Our experiments show that models leak substantially more in multi-party settings than one-to-one evaluations suggest. Frontier models are vulnerable, and smaller open-weights models, often preferred for local deployment with sensitive data, even more so. Existing contextual privacy defences offer only partial protection, degrade utility, and do not resolve the underlying party-tracking problem.

</details>


### [284] [Judgment-Grounded Expansion for Peer Review Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23233)
*Sheng Lu,Lizhen Qu,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"判断引导扩展"（judgment-grounded expansion）的人机协作审稿生成模式，通过结构化生成-检查-精炼流程，将审稿人的评价性主张扩展为完整的审稿评论候选集，并利用保形预测（conformal prediction）平衡候选集大小与目标覆盖率，为未来协作式审稿生成系统提供了实证和方法基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动审稿生成大多采用端到端全自动化模式，缺乏问责机制，难以应用于需要可解释性和人机协作的场景。为了在自动化与问责性之间取得更好平衡，作者提出判断引导扩展这一人机协作范式。

Method: 提出结构化"生成-检查-精炼"（generate-check-refine）流程；通过用户研究收集人机交互数据；开发模拟流程用于大规模评估；采用保形预测（conformal prediction）来平衡候选集规模与目标覆盖率。

Result: 验证了保形预测在平衡候选集大小和覆盖率方面的有效性；建立了大规模评估的方法；证明了判断引导扩展作为具体任务的可行性。

Conclusion: 判断引导扩展作为一种具体的人机协作审稿生成任务被正式确立，本文为未来协作式审稿生成系统的设计提供了实证基础和方法论基础。

Abstract: Automatic review generation is a promising direction for accelerating scientific progress. While most work adopts an end-to-end setup, its fully automated nature makes it less suitable for settings that demand accountability. To better balance automation and accountability, we formalize judgment-grounded expansion, a human-AI collaboration mode where a reviewer provides an evaluative claim and the system expands it into review comment candidate(s). We model it as a structured generate-check-refine process and conduct a user study to collect human-model interaction data. We study two practical challenges for judgment-grounded expansion: scalable evaluation and candidate set curation. We develop methods to simulate the process for large-scale evaluation, and show that conformal prediction is well suited to balancing candidate set size and target coverage. Our work establishes judgment-grounded expansion as a concrete task and provides empirical and methodological foundations for the design of future collaborative review generation systems.

</details>


### [285] [Scaling LLM Knowledge Boundaries via Distribution-Optimized Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23271)
*Songze Li,Yarong Lan,Zhongpu Bo,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhiqiang Liu,Yuan Yuan,Chengtao Gan,Menghao Qian,Enpei Niu,Xiaoke Guo,Yuanxiang Liu,Zhaoyan Gong,Xiangjin Hu,Liangyurui Liu,Jingdian Lu,Lei Liang,Jun Zhou,Huajun Chen,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KDoS框架，从知识分布视角重新审视合成数据驱动的知识注入，通过知识密度驱动的三阶段反馈机制实现分布优化合成，实验证明存在最优知识分布可最大化LLM知识边界扩展，且该分布在不同骨干网络和数据规模下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据方法仅基于预设token数量或固定数据比例进行生成，缺乏对知识分布的意识，导致某些领域稀疏而另一些领域冗余，限制了LLM知识边界的扩展。本文从分布视角重新审视知识注入问题，假设存在最优知识分布可最大化知识边界扩展。

Method: 提出KDoS（Knowledge Distribution-optimized Synthesis）框架，引入知识密度概念，通过三阶段反馈机制驱动合成过程，将合成从盲目生成转变为分布优化合成。基于Wikipedia构建不同知识分布的合成数据，在0.6B到16B规模模型（Qwen、Ling、LLaMA）和1B到5B token数据规模上进行实验。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 存在最优知识分布一致性地最大化知识边界扩展；(2) 该分布在不同骨干网络和模型规模下保持稳定；(3) KDoS在六个知识基准测试中均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为合成数据驱动的知识注入提供了新视角和实用框架，证明了知识分布优化的重要性，KDoS可有效提升LLM知识注入效果。

Abstract: Knowledge injection via synthetic data is crucial for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs). However, current synthesis methods simply stop at preset token counts or fixed data ratios, lacking awareness of knowledge distribution. This results in some domains being sparse while others are redundant, limiting LLM knowledge boundaries. We revisit knowledge injection from a distribution perspective and hypothesize that an optimal knowledge distribution exists to maximize knowledge boundary expansion. We propose KDoS (Knowledge Distribution-optimized Synthesis), a framework that introduces knowledge density to drive synthesis through a three-stage feedback mechanism, shifting from blind generation to distribution-optimized synthesis. We construct Wikipedia-based synthetic data with varying knowledge distributions and conduct experiments on models from 0.6B to 16B (Qwen, Ling, LLaMA) and data scales from 1B to 5B tokens. Our key findings are: (1) an optimal knowledge distribution consistently maximizes boundary expansion; (2) this distribution is stable across backbones and scales; (3) KDoS outperforms baselines across six knowledge benchmarks. Our work offers a new perspective and practical framework for synthetic data-driven knowledge injection.

</details>


### [286] [Towards Root Memories: Benchmarking and Enhancing Implicit Logical Memory Retrieval for Personalized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23283)
*Hongxun Ding,Xiang Yu,Chengbing Wang,Jianfei Xiao,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现有大语言模型记忆系统中仅依赖语义相似性检索而遗漏逻辑关键记忆的问题，构建了首个面向隐式逻辑记忆检索的高质量基准IMLogic，并提出RootMem框架——将用户历史蒸馏为结构化的根记忆(root memory)，通过LLM路由激活逻辑相关记忆，显著提升了检索准确率和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统的检索方法仅依赖语义相似性，可能遗漏语义重叠有限但逻辑关键的记忆，而现有基准不足以评估这一问题，因此需要构建专门针对隐式逻辑记忆检索的基准和解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建IMLogic基准，聚焦长对话场景下的隐式逻辑记忆检索；2) 提出根记忆(root memory)概念，一种结构化的、保留决策信息的表示形式；3) 设计RootMem框架，先蒸馏原始历史为结构化根记忆，再通过LLM路由器激活逻辑相关的记忆，与语义检索互补。

Result: RootMem显著超越了最强的检索基线，并能持续提升现有记忆代理的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过引入根记忆和RootMem框架，可以有效弥补纯语义检索在逻辑推理记忆方面的不足，为构建更个性化的LLM记忆系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Memory systems are essential for personalized Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing retrieval methods in these systems primarily rely on semantic similarity, potentially missing logically critical memories with limited semantic overlap. Current benchmarks remain inadequate for evaluating this problem. To address this gap, we construct IMLogic, the first high-quality benchmark targeting implicit logical memory retrieval in long-dialogue scenarios. Motivated by this challenge, we introduce root memory, a structured, decision-preserving representation that distills reusable personalized logic from long-term user histories. We then propose RootMem, a plug-and-play framework that first distills raw histories into structured root memories and then uses an LLM-based router to activate logically relevant ones, complementing semantic retrieval with personalized decision logic. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RootMem significantly outperforms the strongest retrieval baselines and consistently boosts the accuracy of existing memory agents. Our benchmark and codes will be available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/IMLogic-DBB3.

</details>


### [287] [On the Effect of Segmentation Width and Cluster Size on Speech Resynthesis and Continuation in Generative Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23285)
*Shunsuke Kando,Wataru Nakata,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Yusuke Miyao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式口语语言建模（GSLM）在不同比特率离散语音表示下的语音合成与续接性能，发现较低比特率即可生成可理解且自然的语音，且续接质量在多项指标上保持稳定，表明传统GSLM设置可能冗余。同时，LLM评估指标虽优于传统指标，但与人类评分相关性仍较低，需更稳定的自动评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GSLM在语音合成和续接任务中，使用不同比特率离散语音表示时的性能表现，验证较低比特率是否能够维持生成质量，以及评估传统GSLM设置是否存在冗余。

Method: 使用固定宽度的语音表示分段，训练不同聚类数量的K-means模型以生成多种比特率设置；在多种比特率下进行语音合成和续接实验，对比生成语音的可理解性、自然度以及续接质量。

Result: 较低比特率下仍能合成可理解且自然的语音；语音续接质量在较低比特率下保持稳定；LLM评估指标与人类主观评分相关性高于传统指标，但整体相关性仍然较低。

Conclusion: 传统GSLM的高比特率设置对于有效语音生成可能是冗余的；现有自动评估方法（包括LLM指标）与人类主观评分的相关性仍不足，亟需更稳定的评估方法。

Abstract: Generative Spoken Language Modeling (GSLM) enables text-free speech modeling by training language models (LMs) using discrete speech representations instead of textual transcription. In this paper, we investigate the performance of GSLM on speech synthesis and continuation using discrete speech representations with varying bitrates. We segment speech representations with fixed widths and train K-means models in multiple cluster sizes, resulting in various bitrate settings. We demonstrate that intelligible and natural speech can be synthesized at lower bitrate settings than the baseline. Furthermore, speech continuation quality remains stable at lower bitrates across multiple metrics, suggesting that the conventional GSLM setting may be redundant for effective speech generation. Although LLM-based metrics show higher correlation with human subjective score than conventional metrics, it remains low, highlighting the need for more stable automatic evaluation methods.

</details>


### [288] [The Anatomy of the CTC Oracle Gap: Acoustic Exhaustion and Linguistic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23306)
*Ivan Novosad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统证明CTC内部评分存在信息瓶颈（声学置信度与语言合理性之间的鸿沟），该瓶颈无法通过声学信号重组突破，但可通过外部语言模型（如MBR解码+RoBERTa PLL）有效绕过。同时揭示MWER训练在近收敛检查点失效的根本原因在于训练oracle gap过小导致奖励信号不可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CTC内部评分在N-best假设选择中的极限，定位声学置信度与语言合理性之间的信息瓶颈，并验证该瓶颈的本质是语言性的而非声学性的。

Method: 1) 对比11种CTC内部及声学特征评分策略在贪心解码与N-best重评分上的表现；2) 使用Spearman相关系数分析CTC评分与每语句WER的相关性随beam size变化；3) 引入MBR解码结合RoBERTa伪对数似然(PLL)作为外部语言信息源；4) 在多种架构、领域和噪声条件下验证；5) 从REINFORCE视角分析MWER训练并揭示其失效原因。

Result: 1) 11种CTC内部评分策略在G=16时均无统计显著WER改善；2) CTC的Spearman ρ从G=4的-0.574降至G=128的-0.270（损失53%），归因于空白路径激增；3) MBR-CER+RoBERTa PLL在test-other上达5.42% WER（贪心5.96%），相对改善9.0%；4) RoBERTa PLL的ρ仅退化21%；5) 该方法在11/13条件下显著有效；6) MWER训练在近收敛检查点全部失效（+6.18~+8.90 pp），因训练oracle gap仅0.007 pp。

Conclusion: CTC内部表示具有饱和的判别能力，无法通过声学信号重组缩小oracle gap；瓶颈本质是语言性的，需引入外部语言信息突破；MWER训练在接近收敛时因奖励信号过小导致策略梯度失效。

Abstract: We study the limits of CTC-internal scoring for N-best hypothesis selection and locate the information bottleneck separating acoustic confidence from linguistic plausibility. Eleven CTC-internal and acoustic-feature scoring strategies produce no statistically significant WER improvement over greedy decoding on LibriSpeech dev-other at G=16 (all p > 0.05). The exhaustion is systematic: CTC's Spearman $ρ$ between hypothesis score and per-utterance WER degrades from -0.574 at G=4 to -0.270 at G=128, a 53% loss driven by blank-path proliferation. This establishes that the discriminative capacity of CTC-internal representations is saturated: no recombination of acoustic signals can close the oracle gap.
  Confirming that the bottleneck is linguistic, not acoustic, external linguistic information introduced via MBR decoding breaks through it. MBR-CER decoding with a RoBERTa pseudo-log-likelihood (PLL) posterior ($τ$=10, G=128) achieves 5.42% WER on held-out LibriSpeech test-other (greedy 5.96%, $Δ$=-0.535 pp, p<0.0001, 9.0% relative). RoBERTa PLL $ρ$ degrades only 21% over the same range, retaining discriminating power where CTC loses it. Applied without retuning across two Zipformer architectures, three domains (LibriSpeech, TED-LIUM 3, VoxPopuli), and four MUSAN noise levels, the recipe gives significant gains in 11 of 13 conditions.
  On the training side, standard MWER training via the CTC forward-backward algorithm implements Rao-Blackwellized REINFORCE at the output projection (variance about 3x below Viterbi). Yet sequence-level fine-tuning fails at near-converged checkpoints: all four MWER configurations on CR-CTC collapse (+6.18 to +8.90 pp WER), as a training oracle gap of 0.007 pp provides no usable reward signal.

</details>


### [289] [Tmax: A simple recipe for terminal agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23321)
*Hamish Ivison,Junjie Oscar Yin,Rulin Shao,Teng Xiao,Nathan Lambert,Hannaneh Hajishirzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tmax 是目前最强的开源终端智能体 RL 训练方案，仅用 9B 参数在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上达到 27% 性能，超越先前更大的模型。该方法通过新颖的分类学生成数据（结合难度控制、角色设定和验证器多样化），开源了比先前数据集大 2.5 倍的终端数据集，并采用简单的基于结果的 RL 训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管终端智能体已成为 LM 最流行的下游应用，但学术界对基于 RL 的训练方案研究甚少，主要受限于困难的基准、数据缺乏以及缺少简单易用的基线方案。

Method: 提出 Tmax 配方：1）采用新颖的分类学生成数据，结合难度控制、角色设定和验证器多样化，以低成本大规模生成终端环境；2）开源超过 2.5 倍于先前数据集的终端数据集；3）使用简单的基于结果（outcome-only）的 RL 训练范式训练开源权重模型。

Result: 仅用 9B 参数在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上达到 27% 性能，超越先前工作中更大的模型。

Conclusion: Tmax 为未来终端智能体的开源学术研究提供了一个强大的基线方案，并开源了数据、模型和代码。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents have quickly become the most popular downstream application of language models (LMs). Despite their prevalence, relatively little academic work has examined RL-based training of these models, likely due to difficult benchmarks, a lack of data, and a lack of simple baseline recipes. We present Tmax, the strongest open RL recipe for terminal agents to date, bringing open data recipes closer to the frontier. While simple, our recipe achieves 27\% on Terminal-Bench 2.0 with only 9B parameters, outperforming much larger models from prior work. Concretely, we generate data using a novel taxonomy, combining difficulty control, personas, and verifier diversification, which allows us to cheaply generate large amounts of terminal environments for RL and SFT training. We open-source our terminal dataset, which is over 2.5x larger than previously released terminal-agent datasets. We then train open-weight models using RL with our data, using a simple, outcome-only recipe. We release our data, models, and code as a strong baseline for future open academic work on terminal agents at https://github.com/hamishivi/tmax.

</details>


### [290] [WaveDetect: Robust Framework for Machine-Generated Text Detection via Wavelet Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23336)
*Zhichen Liu,Kaitong Qin,Linhan He,Yang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Wavedetect框架，将LLM生成文本检测重构为时频域信号处理任务，利用可微连续小波变换提取文本的"谱指纹"，在RAID、EvoBench和Domain-Shift三个数据集上达到新SOTA，具备对抗扰动鲁棒性、跨域泛化和对演化模型的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本检测器在面对对抗扰动、跨领域偏移和基础模型快速演化三大挑战时表现脆弱，单纯依赖表面语义伪影的方法日益不可靠，亟需更鲁棒的检测范式。

Method: 将生成文本建模为概率信号，应用可微连续小波变换(CWT)将其转换为可学习的频谱表示，从时频域提取机器生成文本固有的"谱指纹"特征。

Result: 在RAID、EvoBench和Domain-Shift三个数据集上达到SOTA性能，在对抗攻击鲁棒性、跨域泛化和对未见演化LLM的适应性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 频谱分析是LLM生成文本检测的有效新范式，时频域特征相比时域静态概率分布更具鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models asymptotically approach human-level fluency in natural language generation, solely relying on surface-level semantic artifacts for detecting LLM-generated texts has become increasingly precarious. Existing detectors often falter when facing three critical challenges: adversarial perturbations, cross-domain shifts, and the rapid temporal evolution of the foundation model. To address these issues, we propose \wavedetect, a novel framework that reformulates text detection as a signal processing task within the time-frequency domain. Unlike previous methods that analyze static token probability distributions, \wavedetect models the generated output as a probability signal, upon which a differentiable Continuous Wavelet Transform is applied to convert them into learnable spectral representations. This process reveals the intrinsic ``spectral fingerprints'' in machine-generated texts--patterns that remain invisible in time domain. Comprehensive evaluations on three well-curated datasets (RAID, EvoBench, and Domain-Shift) show that our method achieves a new state-of-the-art. It not only achieves superior accuracy but also exhibits remarkable robustness against sophisticated attacks, generalization across out-of-distribution topics and unseen evolving LLMs. Our results validate the efficacy of spectral analysis as a promising paradigm for LLM-generated texts detection.

</details>


### [291] [Measuring & Mitigating Over-Alignment for LLMs in Multilingual Criminal Law Courts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23375)
*Arthur Wuhrmann,Gaetan Stein,Daniel Brunner,Andrei Kucharavy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TF-RefusalBench基准，用于评估刑事法律场景下LLM因过度对齐（over-alignment）导致的拒绝回答和免责声明问题，并证明消融拒绝方向（abliteration）可在不影响任务性能的前提下消除此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在司法领域有应用（如瑞士联邦最高法院使用小型本地模型进行翻译和摘要），但在刑事法律场景中，由于判决和案件常包含暴力和性犯罪的详细描述，模型的防护机制（过度对齐）会拒绝处理或给出免责声明，严重干扰合法工作流程。现有研究缺乏对此现象的量化评估。

Method: 1) 构建TF-RefusalBench多语言基准，包含5,200条提示，覆盖法语、德语、意大利语和英语，涉及刑事法律翻译和摘要任务，并刻意设计易触发拒绝的提示和文本片段。2) 使用该基准评估不同模型在多种语言组合下的拒绝行为。3) 对比评估两类缓解方法：提示工程（prompting）和消融拒绝方向（abliteration）。

Result: 1) 过度对齐是一个多层面现象，受模型本身、提示语言和处理文本语言的共同影响。2) 过度对齐的影响不能仅从过度拒绝率角度评估，因为免责声明还会损害任务忠实度。3) 提示工程虽有一定效果，但消融拒绝方向（abliteration）能完全消除拒绝行为，且对任务性能影响极小。

Conclusion: 在刑事法律等敏感文本场景中，消融拒绝方向是一种比提示工程更有效的缓解过度对齐的方法，能在不牺牲任务质量的前提下解决模型拒绝和免责声明问题。

Abstract: While the wider applicability of LLMs in the legal field is currently debated due to their reliability and the gravity of any errors, narrow uses with well-understood and mitigated risks have emerged. Notably the Swiss Federal Supreme Court uses small on-premises models for tentative translations and short-passage summarization across the four official languages. However, such usage is challenging in the context of Criminal Law. Since rulings and cases employees work on routinely can contain detailed descriptions of violent and sexual offenses, their legitimate work is compromised by refusals and disclaimers due to the activation of model guardrails (over-alignment).
  To measure this phenomenon, we introduce TF-RefusalBench, a multilingual benchmark for criminal-law translation and summarization derived from public Swiss Supreme Court rulings. TF-RefusalBench contains 5,200 total prompts across French, German, Italian, and English, corresponding to common task prompts and passages likely to trigger refusal. We then use TF-RefusalBench to show that over-alignment is a multifaceted phenomenon, influenced by the model and the prompt and text languages being processed, and that its impact cannot be evaluated solely from an over-refusal perspective, given the disclaimer's impact on task faithfulness. Finally, we evaluate approaches to enable on-premises LLMs for Criminal Law Tasks, demonstrating that while prompting can be effective, abliteration (refusal directions ablation) eliminates refusal with minimal impact on task performance.

</details>


### [292] [Energy-Based Transformers as Predictors of Reading Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23382)
*Jakub Dotlacil,Ece Takmaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次探索基于能量的Transformer度量（energy measure）在计算心理语言学中的应用，发现该度量能稳健预测阅读时间，在多项语料库中显著超越surprisal，并在关系从句处理实验中捕捉到主/宾语不对称性，表明能量度量可能统一替代此前所需的多种互补预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Transformer语言模型与Hopfield网络和密集联想记忆模型建立形式化联系，探索基于能量的Transformer度量作为句子处理中统一预测指标的可行性，弥补此前需要surprisal和注意力熵等多个互补指标才能全面刻画处理负荷的不足。

Method: 采用基于能量的Transformer模型，提取能量度量（energy measure）作为阅读难度的预测指标；在Natural Stories、UCL眼动追踪、UCL自定步速阅读三个语料库上进行回归分析，并与surprisal进行对比；在关系从句处理受控实验中检验能量度量对主/宾语不对称性的捕捉能力。

Result: 能量度量在三个阅读时间语料库中均为阅读时间的稳健预测因子，显著优于surprisal；在关系从句处理实验中，单层能量即可捕捉到经典的主/宾语不对称效应；能量度量能够吸收原本由注意力熵和surprisal共同解释的变异。

Conclusion: 能量度量可能作为一个统一的预测指标，替代此前需要多个互补指标才能完成的工作，为句子处理中的认知负荷建模提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Transformer language models have become established tools for modeling human sentence processing, with measures such as surprisal and attention entropy serving as effective predictors of reading difficulty that together capture complementary aspects of processing load. Here, we explore a related class of transformer models: energy-based transformers, which provide a principled formal link to associative memory models, bringing processing research into direct contact with the broader literature on Hopfield networks and dense associative memory. To our knowledge, this is the first exploration of an energy-based transformer measure in computational psycholinguistics. Across reading-time corpora (Natural Stories, UCL eye-tracking, UCL self-paced reading), the energy measure is a robust predictor of reading times, providing significant fit beyond surprisal in all three. In a controlled experiment on relative clause processing, energy at a single layer captures the well-known object/subject asymmetry. We find evidence that it subsumes effects attributable to both attention entropy and surprisal, suggesting that energy may serve as a single unified predictor where multiple complementary measures have previously been required.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [Multimodal Image Colorization: Quantifying the Impact of Text-Conditioned Guidance on Grayscale-to-Color Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20722)
*Colten Reissmann,Hugo Garrido-Lestache Belinchon*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文量化研究了文本条件（CLIP）对灰度图像上色模型的影响，在U-Net和Stable Diffusion 1.5两种架构下，文本条件均显著提升了PSNR、SSIM、色彩度并降低了LPIPS，证明了文本条件对图像上色质量的稳定改进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰度图像上色是计算机视觉中的长期挑战，因为同一灰度输入可能对应多种合理的彩色化结果。现有方法虽有一定进展，但文本条件对图像上色质量的定量影响尚未被系统评估。

Method: 在控制所有其他变量的条件下，分别对U-Net和Stable Diffusion 1.5两种架构，测试有无CLIP文本条件的情况，使用PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS和色彩度指标进行量化对比。

Result: U-Net架构下，文本条件使PSNR提升5.6%、SSIM提升1.2%、色彩度提升36.6%，LPIPS降低7.6%；Stable Diffusion架构下，文本条件使PSNR提升5.8%、SSIM提升1.5%、色彩度提升0.6%，LPIPS降低11.3%。

Conclusion: 文本条件在两种架构规模上均为灰度图像上色质量带来了一致且可量化的改进，验证了文本引导在图像上色任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Grayscale images are commonly found in historical photography restoration, medical imaging, and artistic media. However, automatically applying color to these images remains a significant challenge in computer vision because many plausible colorizations can correspond to the same grayscale input.
  In this work, we quantify the effect of text conditioning on pixel-level and perceptual metrics for grayscale-to-color image models. Specifically, we compare two architectures, a U-Net and Stable Diffusion 1.5, each tested with and without CLIP text conditioning while holding all other variables constant. Our results show that text conditioning improves PSNR by 5.6%, SSIM by 1.2%, and colorfulness by 36.6%, while reducing LPIPS by 7.6% in the U-Net tier. In the Stable Diffusion tier, text conditioning improves PSNR by 5.8%, SSIM by 1.5%, and colorfulness by 0.6%, while reducing LPIPS by 11.3%. These results indicate that text conditioning provides consistent, measurable improvements to colorization quality across both architecture scales.

</details>


### [294] [PIAvatar: Physically Interactive Avatars via Deformation Gradient Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21162)
*Sang-Hun Han,Min-Gyu Park,Jisu Shin,Seunghyun Shin,Jin-Hwi Park,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: PIAvatar是一个同时支持化身-化身和化身-环境物理交互以及非刚性可变形人体仿真的框架，通过解耦运动速度与变形梯度，并在传统物质点法框架内集成骨骼框架，实现物理一致且具备实时跟踪能力的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体化身虽在视觉保真度上取得进展，但缺乏物理能力以支持化身之间及化身与环境的真实交互；已有研究虽尝试引入物理特性，但仅表现出有限的物理变形，导致交互行为受限。

Method: 核心思想是将运动速度与变形梯度解耦；集成骨骼框架实现姿态估计与实时闭环跟踪；在传统物质点法（MPM）框架中实现以确保物理一致性。

Result: 在人体-物体和人体-人体交互场景中评估了该方法在不同交互设置下的行为表现。

Conclusion: PIAvatar通过解耦运动速度与变形梯度并结合骨骼框架与MPM，实现了具备物理意识的化身间及化身与环境间的非刚性交互，克服了现有方法物理变形有限的局限。

Abstract: 3D human avatars have shown impressive visual fidelity driven by pose-conditioned models, yet they still lack the physical ability required for interactions with each other and environments. Although recent studies have made various attempts to incorporate physical characteristics into 3D avatars, they only exhibit limited physical deformations, often leading to constrained interaction behaviors. To resolve this issue, we present PIAvatar, a framework to simultaneously enable physically aware interactions between avatar-avatar and avatar-environment, and a non-rigid deformable human body simulation. In this work, our key insight is to decouple kinematic velocity from deformation gradient. When external forces act on avatars, the kinematic velocity induces stress which hinders the avatar's ability to achieve a desired pose. In addition, we integrate a skeletal framework within the avatar. It allows estimating its poses and real-time tracking in a closed form, even during non-rigid physical interactions. Our approach is implemented within a conventional Material Point Method framework to ensure physically consistent dynamics. We lastly evaluate the method on both human-object and human-human interaction scenarios to assess its behavior under diverse interaction settings.

</details>


### [295] [Scene-Level Heterogeneous Physics Simulation with 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21753)
*Xiaoyang Liu,Shangzhe Wu,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一种表示抽象框架，将3DGS资产、虚拟网格、流体等异构资产统一转换为物理粒子集，实现场景级、异构、多求解器的物理仿真，首次弥合3DGS与物理引擎之间的表示鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在逼真渲染上已达到SOTA，但其表示与物理引擎不兼容，导致资产无法进行物理交互。现有物理-for-3DGS方法局限于物体中心的孤立环境（如理想平面），无法处理复杂静态碰撞几何体或异构资产。

Method: 提出表示抽象框架，将所有异构资产（3DGS、虚拟网格、流体）统一转换为物理粒子集；该粒子集与场景捕获导出的静态碰撞边界一起，由求解器无关的物理内核处理；物理结果再映射回各资产的视觉表示。

Result: 实现了可变形3DGS资产、标准CG资产（流体、网格）与大尺度捕获静态环境之间的复杂双向交互，展示了以前无法实现的真实耦合现象。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了3DGS资产参与场景级、异构、多求解器的物理仿真，突破了现有方法在复杂交互上的根本局限。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has achieved state-of-the-art photorealistic rendering, but the representation gap prevents these assets from being physically interactive. Production-grade physics engines do not understand the 3DGS representation, while prior physics-for-3DGS methods are monolithic silos. These prior works are fundamentally limited, demonstrating only object-centric physics in isolated environments, such as on an ideal plane. They are incapable of interacting with complex static collision geometry or heterogeneous assets. We propose a novel framework that, for the first time, bridges this gap by enabling 3DGS assets to participate in scene-level, heterogeneous, multi-solver physical simulations. Our core contribution is a Representation Abstraction Framework that translates all diverse assets, including 3DGS, virtual meshes, and fluids, into a unified physical particle set. This abstraction is key to enabling complex behaviors, such as the non-rigid deformation of 3DGS assets, within a unified physics pipeline. This particle set, along with the static scene collision boundaries derived from scene capture, is processed within a solver-agnostic physics kernel. The physical results are then mapped back to drive each asset's specific visual reconstruction. This architecture unlocks capabilities impossible with prior art. We demonstrate complex, two-way interactions between deformable 3DGS assets, standard CG assets such as fluids and meshes, and large-scale captured static environments, showcasing realistic coupled phenomena that were previously unattainable.

</details>


### [296] [Single-Event Upsets in 3D Gaussian Splatting Rendering: Bit-Level Criticality, Spatial Extent, and a Parallel Support Guard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21791)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文针对三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）部署于易受瞬态故障影响的硬件（如太空处理器、机器人边缘设备）时的单粒子翻转（SEU）问题，构建了基于GPU的并行故障注入引擎（380万次注入，5.3 GPU小时），发现大部分比特翻转不影响感知质量，但少数高阶位（尤其对数尺度符号位）可导致单个图元膨胀覆盖高达75.7%的帧。基于IEEE-754布局与管线激活函数推导出闭式扰动边界，并据此提出支持守卫（support guard）：对每个图元参数进行训练坐标框裁剪，代价76微秒/帧。768,000次守卫注入下，最坏污染面积限制在11.68%；在20,000次同时翻转的累积剂量下，未守卫渲染器降至10.6 dB，守卫渲染器保持21.8 dB。守卫同时不改变清洁模型、防止帧覆盖污染，并可约束分布式渲染器中污染传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS作为实时场景表示标准，广泛部署于太空处理器、机器人边缘设备等易发生瞬态故障（单粒子翻转）的硬件上，而训练好的模型是GPU内存中大量浮点参数，单个比特翻转即可能导致渲染质量严重退化，现有研究缺乏对3DGS在软错误下的鲁棒性系统评估与防御。

Method: 1) 构建GPU驻留并行故障注入引擎，在4个场景、6个字段、所有比特位置、3种数值格式（fp32/fp16/bf16）上执行380万次受控单比特翻转注入（耗时5.3 GPU小时）；2) 基于IEEE-754浮点布局与管线激活函数推导闭式扰动边界，预测每比特敏感性排序；3) 提出支持守卫：对每个图元参数施加训练坐标框裁剪防护，计算代价仅76微秒/帧。

Result: 1) 大部分翻转因高冗余不改变感知质量，但少量高阶位（尤其对数尺度符号位）可使单个图元膨胀覆盖75.7%帧；2) 闭式扰动边界准确预测比特级敏感性排序；3) 支持守卫在768,000次注入下将最坏污染面积限制在11.68%；4) 20,000次同时翻转累积剂量下，未守卫渲染器PSNR降至10.6 dB，守卫渲染器保持21.8 dB；5) 守卫不改变清洁模型输出。

Conclusion: 3DGS对单粒子翻转具有高度非均匀鲁棒性——大部分翻转无害，少数高阶位灾难性脆弱。支持守卫（训练坐标框裁剪）以微秒级代价有效遏制帧覆盖污染与分布式渲染跨节点传播，可部署于太空、边缘等容错敏感场景。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting is a standard real-time scene representation increasingly deployed on hardware exposed to transient faults, such as spaceborne processors and robotic edge devices where silent data corruption occurs. A trained model is a large array of floating-point parameters in GPU memory, where a single-event upset corresponds to a single flipped bit. This paper measures these effects and constructs a defense. A GPU-resident parallel fault-injection engine applies over 3.8 million controlled single-bit upsets across four scenes, six fields, all bit positions, and three numeric formats (fp32, fp16, bf16), using 5.3 GPU-hours. The effect is highly concentrated: most upsets leave the image perceptually unchanged due to high redundancy, but a small set of high-order bits principally the logarithmic scale's sign bit enlarge a single primitive to cover up to 75.7% of the frame. A closed-form perturbation bound derived from the IEEE-754 layout and pipeline activations predicts this per-bit ordering. This concentration motivates a support guard: a per-primitive clamp of each parameter to the coordinate box observed during training, costing 76 us per frame. Over 768,000 guarded upsets, the worst corruption footprint is restricted to 11.68% of the frame. We prove the guard leaves clean models unchanged and prevents frame-covering corruption. Under an accumulated dose of 20,000 simultaneous upsets, the unguarded renderer degrades to 10.6 dB, whereas the guarded renderer remains at 21.8 dB. The corruption footprint also dictates the number of tile/compositing nodes contaminated in distributed renderers, where the per-node guard contains it.

</details>


### [297] [Mesh2GS: White-Box 3DGS Construction via Plenoptic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21898)
*Haoran Zhu,Youcheng Cai,Huangsheng Du,Jingyang Meng,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Mesh2GS提出了一种基于全光采样理论的白色盒3DGS构建框架，直接从网格几何生成3D高斯分布，实现奈奎斯特级别的高质量全局光照渲染，在实时共享渲染和非朗伯效应捕捉方面超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注从多视角图像进行3DGS到网格的重建，而将网格转换为3DGS的问题受到的关注较少，缺乏理论指导的构建策略。

Method: 1) 基于全光采样理论推导采样视图的最小采样率和3D高斯的分布策略；2) 提出带有反照率-着色分解的3DGS更新流程实现高效全局光照捕捉；3) 引入神经光照增强模块处理非朗伯效应。

Result: 在实时共享渲染和非朗伯效应捕捉（如镜面高光）方面超越了现有的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: Mesh2GS为从网格生成3DGS提供了理论驱动的白盒框架，实现了高质量的全局光照渲染效果。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising method for high-quality, real-time 3D reconstruction. To associate 3DGS with mesh representations, existing methods primarily focus on 3DGS-to-mesh reconstruction from multi-view images. In contrast, the problem of converting a mesh into 3DGS has received comparatively less attention. Instead of relying on heuristic strategies that bind 3D Gaussians to the mesh, we propose a novel white-box 3DGS construction framework, termed Mesh2GS, which generates 3DGS directly from mesh geometry based on plenoptic sampling theory, achieving Nyquist-level performance for high-quality global illumination rendering. Firstly, we propose a plenoptic sampling guided 3DGS construction strategy that theoretically derives the minimum sampling rate of the sampled views and the distribution of 3D Gaussians. Second, we propose a novel 3DGS update procedure with albedo--shading decomposition for efficient global-illumination capture. Finally, we introduce a neural illumination enhancement module to handle non-Lambertian effects. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses state-of-the-art baselines and is practically effective for both real-time shared rendering and non-Lambertian effects capturing specular highlights. The project code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [298] [Lighting-Consistent Object Transfer Across Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22481)
*Nicolás Violante,George Kopanas,Linus Franke,Julien Philip,George Drettakis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）对象合成光照协调方法，通过训练扩散模型对光照不一致的合成图像进行协调，并结合后优化步骤将协调后的多视图整合为一致的3DGS场景，实现了不同光照条件下3DGS场景间对象的高质量转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS广泛应用于真实场景的捕获与渲染，将对象从一个场景合成到另一个场景在VFX、建筑设计、营销等领域具有重要应用价值。然而，直接粘贴对象会因两场景光照条件不同而产生不真实的结果，现有方法缺乏有效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出一个扩散模型，用于协调光照不一致的合成图像；2) 使用混合数据集（包含合成数据、生成数据和真实数据）训练该模型；3) 完整3D解决方案：用户从源场景提取对象并合成到目标场景→渲染目标场景中带有合成对象的不一致视图→扩散模型协调每个视图→通过后优化步骤将协调后的视图整合为3DGS表示。

Result: 该方法提供了视觉上令人信服的结果，使3DGS之间的对象转移易于使用，与前方法相比显著提高了质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效解决了3DGS场景间对象转移的光照不一致问题，通过扩散模型协调和后优化整合，实现了高质量、易用的对象合成效果。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is widely used to capture and render real scenes. Compositing objects from one capture into another has applications in many domains, such as VFX, architecture and interior design, or marketing. However, extracting an object from a source scene and naively pasting it into a target scene will fail to produce realistic results due to the different lighting conditions between the two scenes. To address this problem, we introduce a diffusion model that harmonizes naively composited images with inconsistent lighting. The model is trained with a heterogeneous dataset of image pairs (inconsistent composite input, consistent output), combining synthetic, generated, and real data. Our complete 3D solution allows a user to extract an object from the source scene and composite it into the target scene. From this, the (inconsistent) views of the target scene with the composite object are rendered. Our diffusion model harmonizes each one of these views, which are finally consolidated in a 3DGS representation with a post-optimization step. Our method provides visually compelling results, making object transfer between 3DGS easy to use and significantly improving quality compared to previous methods.

</details>


### [299] [Line Drawings using LightBenders: Authoring and Illuminating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22499)
*Hamed Alimohammadzadeh,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LightBender的无人机系统，通过软硬件协同设计，实现空中绘制线条图形和文字符号的照明效果，支持动画和矢量图形导入，并通过用户实验验证了系统在10.1毫米最大偏差下感知质量中位数为8/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中照明系统在绘制复杂线条图形和文字符号方面缺乏灵活性，难以实现精确、可动画化的空中光照表达。本研究旨在开发一套完整的软硬件系统，使无人机集群能够在室内空间中精准绘制线条图形和文字符号的空中照明效果。

Method: 硬件方面设计了搭载伺服驱动关节和密集可寻址LED灯带的无人机(LightBender)；软件方面包括：1) 估算所需LightBender最小数量的算法与启发式方法、缓解下沉气流的编队策略、生成Swarm Flight and Lighting (SFL)文件及执行控制；2) Blender插件实现注册、创作和部署功能；3) 支持SVG矢量图形导入的独立工具LB-Author。通过IRB批准的21人用户实验评估对准偏差对感知影响。

Result: 系统在最大10.1毫米的对准偏差下，用户感知质量中位数为8/10，证明该偏差在感知上可接受。

Conclusion: 所提出的LightBender系统能够有效实现室内空中线条图形和文字符号的照明，其对准误差在感知可接受范围内，为空中照明提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This study presents the hardware and software architecture of a transformative system for illuminating line drawings and letterforms. These mid-air illuminations are indoors and might be animated. The hardware contribution is a drone equipped with servo-actuated rod joints and a dense, addressable LED strip that enables arbitrary orientation, a LightBender. The software contributions are threefold. First, the system implements algorithms and heuristics to estimate the minimum number of LightBenders required to render a line drawing or letterform, stagger swarm formations to mitigate LightBender downwash, generate Swarm Flight and Lighting (SFL) files, and execute these files using a swarm of LightBenders to illuminate line drawings and letterforms. Second, a Blender add-on enables users to register LightBenders, author graphics and animations represented by swarms of LightBenders, and deploy the swarm for illumination through one-click functions. Third, users may import SVG files into either the Blender add-on or a standalone LB-Author tool to illuminate line drawings directly from vector graphics.
  We present results from an IRB-approved human subject study (n=21) to evaluate the impact of LightBender misalignment on the perceived illuminations. Obtained results demonstrate that the system's 10.1 mm maximum misalignment is perceptually acceptable across tested illuminations, with a median quality rating of 8 on a 0-10 scale.

</details>


### [300] [Illuminating English Letters Using a Flying Light Speck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22592)
*Hamed Alimohammadzadeh,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一种飞行光斑（FLS）系统，通过机载摄像头和计算实现英文字母的照明，实验表明42-56毫米的定位误差影响字母检测，且字母照明呈现顺序显著影响检测时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用飞行光斑（FLS）实现字母照明的能力，并量化其性能表现及影响因素。

Method: 设计FLS系统，利用机载摄像头和计算进行定位并沿轨迹飞行照明字母；通过定量误差测量和定性人类受试者实验（20名参与者，经IRB批准）进行评估。

Result: 定位误差为42-56毫米，该误差影响字母的检测；字母照明呈现顺序对检测时长有显著影响。

Conclusion: FLS能够实现字母照明，但存在毫米级定位误差，且实验设计中的呈现顺序是需要考虑的重要因素。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and implementation of a Flying Light Speck (FLS) to illuminate English letters. The FLS uses its onboard camera and computing to localize and follow a trajectory to illuminate a letter. We evaluate the illuminations quantitatively and qualitatively. The latter is based on an IRB approved human subject study with 20 participants. The obtained results show a 42 to 56 millimeter error that impacts the detection of letters. A key finding is that the order in which the illumination of letters is presented to subjects has a significant effect on detection duration.

</details>


### [301] [DJM: Compact Base Meshes for Displacement Mapping using Triangle Jacobians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22880)
*Congyi Zhang,Nicholas Vining,Yanhong Lin,Alireza Khatami,Ziyu Sun,Xiaohu Guo,Wenping Wang,Alla Sheffer*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出基于位移雅可比度量(DJM)的基网格构造方法，通过约束位移映射的双射性和低失真性，在粗基网格上实现高精度几何重建，优于现有方法在精度与尺寸之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将复杂三角形网格表示为粗基网格上的位移函数可实现紧凑存储和加速渲染，但现有方法在计算基网格时难以在保持低三角形数量的同时实现高精度逼近，核心挑战在于如何获得低参数化失真的双射位移映射。

Method: 提出DJM度量，通过逐点雅可比矩阵评估位移函数失真；基于QEM简化方案引入位移映射的双射性和低失真约束（雅可比下界）；通过显式存储输入网格与基网格之间的映射，利用鲁棒的逆重心位移求解器获得稠密对应关系，避免不可靠的射线-网格求交。

Result: 在重建精度与尺寸的权衡上优于现有方案；在微网格渲染和神经编码任务中展示了鲁棒性和可用性。

Conclusion: 基于雅可比度量的基网格构造方法能有效指导基网格计算，在保持紧凑性的同时提高重建精度，适用于微网格渲染和神经表示等下游应用。

Abstract: Representing complex geometry as a displacement function defined over a coarse base mesh enables compact storage and accelerated rendering. The core challenge in converting detailed triangle meshes into this representation is computing base meshes that have as few triangles as possible, while also supporting displacement functions that accurately approximate the input. Accurate approximation requires the supported displacement functions to bijectively map the input surface onto the base with low parametric distortion. We observe that this distortion can be measured by evaluating the pointwise Jacobian of the displacement functions. Our new DJM (Displacement Jacobian Metric)-based base-mesh construction method uses the Jacobian of the displacement functions to guide base mesh computation, enabling us to outperform prior approaches in terms of accuracy to size trade-off. We achieve this goal by proposing a variant of the QEM-based simplification scheme that constrains the displacement mapping between the input and the base to be bijective and low distortion (defined as satisfying a lower bound on the mapping Jacobian). When evaluating and encoding the displacement maps, we avoid unreliable ray-mesh intersections by explicitly storing the mapping between the input mesh and the base throughout the construction process, and use this mapping within a robust inverse barycentric displacement solver to obtain dense base-to-mesh correspondences to assist all computations. We demonstrate DJM to outperform alternative schemes in terms of reconstruction accuracy to size trade-off, and demonstrate its robustness and usability for micromesh-based rendering and neural encoding.

</details>


### [302] [Controllable Texture Tiling with Transformed RoPE-Enhanced Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22945)
*Junrong Huang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Rui Tang,Hongbo Fu,Jnig Liao*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散变换器（Diffusion Transformers）的可控高保真纹理平铺框架，通过坐标变换旋转位置嵌入（CT-RoPE）实现精确的空间操控，并利用分离注意力掩码（Disjoint Attention Mask）防止语义泄露，在保持场景光照和几何一致性的同时，实现用户指定纹理的精准重复平铺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材质迁移和参考引导修复方法无法满足纹理平铺任务中按用户指定参数（频率、方向、尺度）精确重复参考图案的需求，且生成方法常因像素级重采样破坏结构保真度，或缺乏细粒度空间信息，难以保持原始场景的光照和几何一致性。

Method: 提出两种关键技术：1) 坐标变换旋转位置嵌入（CT-RoPE），将2D仿射变换直接应用于目标潜变量与图像条件之间的相对位置嵌入，无需显式像素扭曲即可精确控制平铺模式；2) 分离注意力掩码，屏蔽参考特征中的语义泄露，在保持结构完整性的同时无缝融合合成纹理与场景的原始光照和几何。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在控制精度和纹理保真度方面均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于扩散变换器的框架通过解耦空间操控与内容生成，有效解决了纹理平铺中精确控制和结构保真度的难题，同时保持了场景一致性。

Abstract: Realistic integration of user-specified textures into scene images is a fundamental task in computer graphics and image editing. While existing material transfer and reference-guided inpainting methods can edit surface appearances, they often fail to address the specific requirements of texture tiling. This task necessitates precisely repeating a reference pattern according to user-defined parameters such as frequency, orientation, and scale. Furthermore, current generative approaches often struggle to maintain the structural fidelity of the reference texture, limited by either destructive pixel-level resampling or the lack of fine-grained spatial information in semantic image encoders, and they frequently fail to preserve the coherent lighting and geometry of the original scene. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for controllable and high-fidelity texture tiling based on Diffusion Transformers. Our approach introduces two key technical innovations to decouple spatial manipulation from content generation. First, we propose a Coordinate-Transformed Rotary Embedding mechanism. By applying 2D affine transformations directly to the relative positional embeddings between the target latent and the image condition, we achieve precise control over tiling patterns without explicit pixel warping, thereby utilizing the full information of the reference condition without degradation. Second, a Disjoint Attention Mask is employed to shield reference features from semantic leakage. This preserves structural integrity while seamlessly blending the synthesized texture with the scene's original lighting and geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both control accuracy and texture fidelity.

</details>


### [303] [VolHuMe: a High-Resolution Large Scale Dataset of Volumetric Human Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23062)
*Giulia Martinelli,Niccolò Bisagno,Nicola Garau,Esa Rahtu,Nicola Conci*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: VolHuMe是一个高质量4D人体扫描数据集，包含104名受试者，使用64个RGB和32个深度相机在容积工作室采集，提供了SMPL-X、高分辨率网格、多视图RGB/深度图像、服装分割等丰富标注，并在3D/4D人体重建任务上进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集主要依赖全身图像，缺乏保留精细身体部位细节的高分辨率、近距采集方案，限制了人体重建的几何保真度和纹理分辨率评估。

Method: 使用64个RGB和32个深度相机构成的容积采集系统，对104名受试者进行近距高分辨率4D扫描，生成包含SMPL-X参数模型、高分辨率网格、多视图RGB/深度图像、绑定网格、点云、服装分割及手部/面部精细几何在内的综合标注。

Result: 在3D和4D人体重建任务上对现有SOTA方法进行了基准测试，展示了数据集的高质量，并暴露了当前评估基准的局限性。

Conclusion: VolHuMe通过提供高保真度的精细化人体扫描数据，弥补了现有数据集在精细身体部位重建评估方面的不足，为人体数字重建研究提供了更严格的评测基准。

Abstract: We introduce VolHuMe, a dataset of high-quality 4D human scans captured with a state-of-the-art volumetric studio using 64 RGB and 32 depth cameras. VolHuMe contains individual captures of 104 subjects and provides extensive ground truth, including SMPL-X, high-resolution meshes, multi-view RGB/depth images, rigged meshes, point clouds, garment segmentation, and detailed hand and facial geometry. Unlike prior datasets that primarily rely on full-body imagery, VolHuMe uses a close-range, high-resolution capture setup that preserves fine-grained body-part details, improving geometric fidelity and texture resolution. We benchmark VolHuMe on state-of-the-art methods across 3D and 4D human reconstruction tasks, showcasing the dataset's quality and exposing the limitations of current evaluation testbeds.

</details>


### [304] [MeshFlow: Mesh Generation with Equivariant Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23489)
*Qi Sun,Kiyohiro Nakayama,Jing Nathan Yan,Qixing Huang,Alexander Rush,Leonidas Guibas,Gordon Wetzstein,Jing Liao,Guandao Yang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MeshFlow提出了一种基于等变最优传输流匹配的三角网格直接生成方法，通过修改扩散Transformer架构保持网格的置换对称性，在保证生成质量的同时实现约18倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格表示具有面置换不变性和顶点置换不变性等关键对称性，现有方法通常将网格序列化为长自回归序列，破坏了这些对称性且推理效率低下。MeshFlow旨在直接以三角面片集合（triangle soups）形式生成网格，同时保持对排列对称性的等变性。

Method: 采用等变最优传输流匹配模型；对扩散Transformer（DiT）架构进行简单有效的修改以实现所需等变性；引入基于最优传输的训练目标，通过消除违反对称性的监督信号来改善收敛。

Result: 生成质量与最先进的自回归网格生成器相当，推理速度提升约18倍。

Conclusion: 通过等变流匹配直接生成三角网格是可行且高效的，避免序列化操作和保持对称性可显著提升推理速度而不牺牲质量。

Abstract: Meshes are among the most common 3D scene representations, but directly generating meshes is challenging because the representation contains important symmetries, including permutation invariance of faces and vertices. MeshFlow learns to generate triangle meshes directly as triangle soups, avoiding the need to serialize meshes into long autoregressive sequences. We adopt equivariant optimal-transport flow matching models that respect the key symmetries of triangle soups: arbitrary permutations of faces and permutations of the vertices within each face.
  Toward this goal, we propose a simple yet effective modification to the Diffusion Transformer architecture, resulting in a scalable network capable of modeling a velocity field while maintaining the desired equivariance. We further introduce an optimal-transport-based training objective that improves convergence by eliminating supervision signals that violate these symmetries. MeshFlow achieves mesh quality comparable to state-of-the-art autoregressive mesh generators while providing about an 18$\times$ speedup during inference. Project page is at https://qiisun.github.io/MeshFlow/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [305] [On the Identifiability of User Adaptation in Co-Adaptive Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20569)
*Philip Waggoner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了共适应人机系统中的可识别性问题，指出闭环编码器估计无法唯一识别用户适应性，而是反映联合系统的特性，并讨论了行为适应性解读的含义及识别条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共适应人机系统中，用户适应性估计存在可识别性问题，即观察到的行为变化可能由多种潜在因素（如用户策略、系统动态、交互耦合）共同导致，现有方法难以区分这些因素的独立贡献。

Method: 通过理论分析闭环系统中编码器估计的数学性质，证明在用户与机器相互适应的闭环条件下，编码器估计值无法唯一分解为用户适应性的独立分量，而是耦合了人机双方动态特性的联合表征。

Result: 揭示了闭环编码器估计的本质是联合系统状态的混合度量，而非用户适应性的纯量；若直接以此推断用户行为适应机制，将导致有偏或误导性结论。

Conclusion: 为从观测数据中正确分离用户适应性，需设计满足特定可识别性条件的实验或算法（如引入外部扰动、施加结构约束），从而保证对行为适应性机制的因果解读有效。

Abstract: We analyze identifiability in co-adaptive human-machine systems. We show that closed-loop encoder estimates do not uniquely identify user adaptation, but instead reflect properties of the joint system. We discuss implications for interpreting behavioral adaptation and propose conditions for identification.

</details>


### [306] [Beyond Fixed Budgets: Characterizing the Inelasticity and Limitations of Tree-of-Thought Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20599)
*Atkia Mahila,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种代表性思维树搜索方法（DPTS和SSDP）在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下的表现，发现DPTS在低预算时存在冷启动瓶颈，而SSDP则容易陷入前沿枯竭，表明固定探索或固定剪枝策略无法适应计算资源连续变化的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维树搜索方法在实践中如何在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下表现，这一关键问题尚未得到系统性的关注和研究。

Method: 在Math500和GSM8K两个数学推理基准上，使用Llama-3B和Llama-8B两种模型规模，设置4种token预算（3k-10k），对比评估DPTS（基于蒙特卡洛树搜索）和SSDP（基于语义去重）两种代表性思维树搜索方法。

Result: DPTS在低预算时因需要足够探索才能获得可靠估值而存在冷启动瓶颈，但在高预算时扩展性良好；SSDP能高效到达候选解但因激进的节点合并永久丢弃未探索路径，导致前沿枯竭，无法随预算增加而进一步改善。

Conclusion: 固定探索策略或固定剪枝策略均无法在整个计算资源连续谱上表现良好，科学推理代理的有效搜索需要基于搜索进度和可用资源自适应的策略。

Abstract: Tree of Thought (ToT) search has become a promising direction for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but deploying these methods in practice raises a question that has received little systematic attention: how do different search strategies behave under varying compute budgets, model sizes, and problem difficulties? In this work, we evaluate two representative ToT methods; DPTS, a Monte Carlo tree search based approach, and SSDP, a semantic deduplication based approach, across two mathematical reasoning benchmarks (Math500 and GSM8K), two model scales (Llama-3B and Llama-8B), and four token budgets (3k--10k). Our analysis reveals that the two methods exhibit limitations that pull in opposite directions. DPTS suffers from a cold-start bottleneck at low budgets: it requires sufficient exploration before its value estimates become reliable, making it a poor fit for resource-constrained settings despite strong scaling behavior at higher budgets. SSDP, on the other hand, reaches candidate solutions efficiently but is prone to frontier depletion; its aggressive node merging permanently discards unexplored paths, leaving it unable to improve regardless of how much budget remains. Together, these findings suggest that neither a fixed exploration strategy nor a fixed pruning strategy is sufficient across compute continuum. We argue that effective search for scientific reasoning agents requires strategies that can adapt their behavior based on search progress and available resources.

</details>


### [307] [The New Associationism: Lessons from Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20600)
*Daniel Rothschild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现代AI的成功支持一种温和的联想主义，即监督学习（由评价性反馈驱动的学习）作为统一、渐进、错误驱动的学习机制，是当代AI系统（从大语言模型到游戏智能体）的核心，这反驳了联想主义机制过于局限的观点；但深度学习依赖于超越经典联想主义的计算架构，监督学习只是其中一部分而非完整解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现代AI的成功对人类学习机制的理论启示，特别是检验联想主义在认知科学中的合理性，并反驳"联想主义过于局限"这一历史性批评。

Method: 通过分析当代AI系统（大语言模型、游戏智能体等）的共同核心——监督学习（由评价性反馈驱动的学习），论证其与联想主义原则的一致性，同时指出深度学习架构对经典联想主义的扩展。

Result: 发现监督学习是当代AI系统的核心机制，验证了联想主义关于统一、渐进、错误驱动的跨领域学习机制的主张，但深度学习架构超越了经典联想主义的设想范围。

Conclusion: 现代AI的成功支持一种温和的联想主义：监督学习作为核心机制具有解释力，但不能作为学习的完整解释，需结合更复杂的计算架构。

Abstract: What can the success of modern AI tell us about how humans learn? This paper argues that taking AI seriously as a model of human learning supports a modest but genuine associationism. The central finding is that supervised learning -- learning driven by evaluative feedback -- underlies a surprisingly wide range of contemporary AI systems, from large language models to game-playing agents, differing primarily in how much work is required to generate the relevant feedback signal. This vindicates associationist ideals of a uniform, gradual, error-driven learning mechanism operating across domains, and defuses the once-influential argument that associationist mechanisms are too limited to account for human cognitive capacities. At the same time, the successes of deep learning depend on computational architectures that go well beyond anything classical associationists envisaged, and supervised learning operates within these as one component rather than a complete account of learning.

</details>


### [308] [Specifying AI-SDLC Processes: A Protocol Language for Human-Agent Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20615)
*Ylli Prifti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI-SDLC流程的领域特定语言（DSL），用于表达人机协作中的责任边界、审批关卡和治理约束。该语言区分策略与机制，通过验证令牌和能力边界实现结构强制执行，并形式化了2+N团队模式与职责分离原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体作为软件开发生命周期的一等成员参与协作，但缺乏专门的语言来表达人机之间的责任边界、审批关卡和治理约束。现有方法要么将流程编码在智能体提示中（易漂移），要么针对相邻领域（工作流管理、业务流程），或仅处理碎片化问题（访问控制、审批关卡）。

Method: 提出一种领域特定语言，具有形式化语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变量。核心设计是将策略（声明意图）与机制（结构强制执行）相分离，通过验证令牌和能力边界等原语约束流程非确定性。引入2+N团队模式（两个人类控制角色+N个专业智能体成员）形式化AI-SDLC中的经典职责分离。

Result: 失效速率分析表明：结构强制执行将系统失效率限定在智能体和验证器速率的加权乘积上，而行为合规性则导致累积或接近饱和的增长。2+N团队模式形式化了职责分离。Kleene闭包的编排循环和自反性协议遵守验证作为设计属性而非特例构造出现。

Conclusion: 该方法相对于多智能体框架（MetaGPT）、工作流规范（FlowAgent、BPMN扩展）和基于能力的安全（SAGA）的创新点在于特定集成，而非单一原语。已有工作实现证明可行性，实证评估为未来工作。

Abstract: AI agents now participate as first-class team members across the software development lifecycle, yet no specification language exists for expressing the human-agent responsibility boundaries, approval gates, and governance constraints this collaboration requires. Existing approaches encode process in agent prompts (subject to drift), target adjacent domains (workflow management, business processes), or address only fragments (access control, approval gates).
  We propose a domain-specific language for specifying AI-SDLC processes as protocols, with formal syntax, well-formedness conditions, operational semantics, and enforcement invariants. The language distinguishes policy (declared intent) from mechanism (structural enforcement), enabling implementations to bound process non-determinism through primitives such as validation tokens and capability boundaries.
  Three results follow. A failure rate analysis shows that structural enforcement bounds system failure rates at a weighted product of agent and validator rates, while behavioral compliance permits cumulative or near-saturating growth. The 2+N team pattern (two human-in-control roles plus N specialized agent members) formalizes classical Separation of Duties for AI-SDLC. Kleene closure of orchestration loops and reflexive protocol-adherence validation emerge as design properties rather than special-case constructs.
